import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

/// Represents a classified batch of units under a single quantified asset
class AssetQuantityDistribution {
  final String utilizationStatus; // 'In Used', 'In Storage', 'Disposed'
  final String physicalCondition; // 'Excellent', 'Good', 'Fair', 'Poor', 'Damaged'
  final int quantity;

  const AssetQuantityDistribution({
    required this.utilizationStatus,
    required this.physicalCondition,
    required this.quantity,
  });

  static const List<String> standardUtilizationStatuses = [
    'In Used',
    'In Storage',
    'Disposed',
  ];

  static const List<String> standardPhysicalConditions = [
    'Excellent',
    'Good',
    'Fair',
    'Poor',
    'Damaged',
  ];

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'status': utilizationStatus,
      'condition': physicalCondition,
      'quantity': quantity,
    };
  }

  factory AssetQuantityDistribution.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    final rawStatus = map['status']?.toString() ?? 'In Used';
    String normStatus = 'In Used';
    final lower = rawStatus.toLowerCase();
    if (lower.contains('storage') || lower.contains('available') || lower.contains('reserved')) {
      normStatus = 'In Storage';
    } else if (lower.contains('disposed') || lower.contains('decommissioned')) {
      normStatus = 'Disposed';
    } else if (lower.contains('use') || lower == 'active') {
      normStatus = 'In Used';
    }

    return AssetQuantityDistribution(
      utilizationStatus: normStatus,
      physicalCondition: map['condition']?.toString() ?? 'Good',
      quantity: int.tryParse(map['quantity']?.toString() ?? '') ?? 1,
    );
  }

  String get summary => '$quantity $utilizationStatus ($physicalCondition)';
}

class AssetAuditLogModel {
  final String auditId;
  final String assetId;
  final String controlNumber;
  final String? auditedBy;
  final String? auditorName;
  final String? previousCondition;
  final String newCondition;
  final String? previousStatus;
  final String? newStatus;
  final String? previousLocation;
  final String? newLocation;
  final String auditMethod; // 'QR_SCAN', 'RFID_NFC', 'MANUAL'
  final String? auditNotes;
  final DateTime auditedAt;

  // Quantity Tracking and Adjustment History
  final int? previousQuantity;
  final int? newQuantity;
  final int? quantityChange;
  final String? quantityChangeReason;

  // Quantified Asset Condition & Utilization Distribution
  final List<AssetQuantityDistribution>? quantityDistribution;

  const AssetAuditLogModel({
    required this.auditId,
    required this.assetId,
    required this.controlNumber,
    this.auditedBy,
    this.auditorName,
    this.previousCondition,
    required this.newCondition,
    this.previousStatus,
    this.newStatus,
    this.previousLocation,
    this.newLocation,
    this.auditMethod = 'QR_SCAN',
    this.auditNotes,
    required this.auditedAt,
    this.previousQuantity,
    this.newQuantity,
    this.quantityChange,
    this.quantityChangeReason,
    this.quantityDistribution,
  });

  bool get hasQuantityAdjustment =>
      quantityChange != null && quantityChange != 0;

  bool get hasQuantityDistribution =>
      quantityDistribution != null && quantityDistribution!.isNotEmpty;

  String get formattedQuantityChange {
    if (!hasQuantityAdjustment) return 'No quantity change';
    final sign =
    quantityChange! > 0 ? '+${quantityChange!}' : '${quantityChange!}';
    return '$sign units (from $previousQuantity to $nextQty)';
  }

  int? get nextQty => newQuantity;

  /// Formatted breakdown summary for audit log tiles
  String get formattedDistributionSummary {
    if (!hasQuantityDistribution) return '';
    return quantityDistribution!
        .map((d) => '${d.quantity}x ${d.utilizationStatus} (${d.physicalCondition})')
        .join(', ');
  }

  /// Clean user notes without the automated metadata headers
  String get cleanNotes {
    if (auditNotes == null) return '';
    String notes = auditNotes!;

    // 1. Clean QTY_ADJUST header
    notes = notes.replaceAll(RegExp(r'\[QTY_ADJUST:.*?\]\n?'), '');

    // 2. Clean QTY_DISTRIBUTION header handling nested JSON array brackets
    const prefix = '[QTY_DISTRIBUTION:';
    while (notes.contains(prefix)) {
      final startIndex = notes.indexOf(prefix);
      final jsonStart = notes.indexOf('[', startIndex + prefix.length);

      if (jsonStart != -1) {
        int depth = 0;
        int jsonEnd = -1;
        for (int i = jsonStart; i < notes.length; i++) {
          if (notes[i] == '[') depth++;
          if (notes[i] == ']') {
            depth--;
            if (depth == 0) {
              jsonEnd = i;
              break;
            }
          }
        }

        if (jsonEnd != -1) {
          final outerEnd = notes.indexOf(']', jsonEnd + 1);
          final endOfTag = outerEnd != -1 ? outerEnd + 1 : jsonEnd + 1;
          notes = notes.substring(0, startIndex) + notes.substring(endOfTag);
        } else {
          notes = notes.replaceFirst(RegExp(r'\[QTY_DISTRIBUTION:.*?\]\n?'), '');
        }
      } else {
        notes = notes.replaceFirst(RegExp(r'\[QTY_DISTRIBUTION:.*?\]\n?'), '');
      }
    }

    return notes.trim();
  }

  String get formattedAuditedAt {
    return '${auditedAt.year}-${auditedAt.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${auditedAt.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')} '
        '${auditedAt.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${auditedAt.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  factory AssetAuditLogModel.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    String? auditor;
    if (map['users'] is Map) {
      final u = map['users'];
      auditor = '${u['first_name'] ?? ''} ${u['last_name'] ?? ''}'.trim();
    }

    final rawNotes = map['audit_notes']?.toString();

    // 1. Parse automated quantity adjustment metadata from audit notes
    int? prevQty;
    int? nextQty;
    int? changeQty;
    String? reasonQty;

    if (rawNotes != null && rawNotes.contains('[QTY_ADJUST:')) {
      final match = RegExp(
        r'\[QTY_ADJUST: from (\d+) to (\d+) \(([+-]?\d+)\) \| REASON: (.*?)\]',
      ).firstMatch(rawNotes);

      if (match != null) {
        prevQty = int.tryParse(match.group(1) ?? '');
        nextQty = int.tryParse(match.group(2) ?? '');
        changeQty = int.tryParse(match.group(3) ?? '');
        reasonQty = match.group(4)?.trim();
      }
    }

    // 2. Parse quantity-based distribution breakdown using bracket depth matching
    List<AssetQuantityDistribution>? parsedDistribution;
    if (rawNotes != null && rawNotes.contains('[QTY_DISTRIBUTION:')) {
      try {
        const prefix = '[QTY_DISTRIBUTION:';
        final startIndex = rawNotes.indexOf(prefix);
        final jsonStart = rawNotes.indexOf('[', startIndex + prefix.length);

        if (jsonStart != -1) {
          int depth = 0;
          int jsonEnd = -1;
          for (int i = jsonStart; i < rawNotes.length; i++) {
            if (rawNotes[i] == '[') depth++;
            if (rawNotes[i] == ']') {
              depth--;
              if (depth == 0) {
                jsonEnd = i;
                break;
              }
            }
          }

          if (jsonEnd != -1) {
            final jsonStr = rawNotes.substring(jsonStart, jsonEnd + 1);
            final List<dynamic> list = jsonDecode(jsonStr);
            parsedDistribution = list
                .map((item) => AssetQuantityDistribution.fromMap(
                item as Map<String, dynamic>))
                .toList();
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        debugPrint('Notice parsing quantity distribution: $e');
      }
    }

    return AssetAuditLogModel(
      auditId: map['audit_id']?.toString() ?? '',
      assetId: map['asset_id']?.toString() ?? '',
      controlNumber: map['control_number']?.toString() ?? '',
      auditedBy: map['audited_by']?.toString(),
      auditorName: auditor,
      previousCondition: map['previous_condition']?.toString(),
      newCondition: map['new_condition']?.toString() ?? 'VERIFIED / GOOD',
      previousStatus: map['previous_status']?.toString(),
      newStatus: map['new_status']?.toString(),
      previousLocation: map['previous_location']?.toString(),
      newLocation: map['new_location']?.toString(),
      auditMethod: map['audit_method']?.toString() ?? 'QR_SCAN',
      auditNotes: rawNotes,
      auditedAt: DateTime.tryParse(map['audited_at']?.toString() ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
      previousQuantity: prevQty,
      newQuantity: nextQty,
      quantityChange: changeQty,
      quantityChangeReason: reasonQty,
      quantityDistribution: parsedDistribution,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'audit_id': auditId,
      'asset_id': assetId,
      'control_number': controlNumber,
      'audited_by': auditedBy,
      'previous_condition': previousCondition,
      'new_condition': newCondition,
      'previous_status': previousStatus,
      'new_status': newStatus,
      'previous_location': previousLocation,
      'new_location': newLocation,
      'audit_method': auditMethod,
      'audit_notes': auditNotes,
      'audited_at': auditedAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }
}

// =============================================================================

import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'asset_model.dart';

class AssetDraftModel {
  final String draftId;
  final String itemName;
  final String? locationId;
  final String locationAcronym;
  final String? locationName;
  final String? classificationId;
  final String classificationAcronym;
  final String? classificationName;
  final String sequenceNumber;
  final int quantity;
  final double unitPrice;
  final double totalCost;
  final DateTime dateOfAcquisition;
  final String modeOfAcquisition;
  final String conditionStatus;
  final String operationalStatus;
  final String? dimensions;
  final String? color;
  final String? model;
  final String? others;
  final String? remarks;
  final String? rfidTag;
  final String? photoUrl;
  final Uint8List? localPhotoBytes;
  final String? photoFileName;
  final String? createdBy;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  AssetDraftModel({
    required this.draftId,
    required this.itemName,
    this.locationId,
    required this.locationAcronym,
    this.locationName,
    this.classificationId,
    required this.classificationAcronym,
    this.classificationName,
    required this.sequenceNumber,
    this.quantity = 1,
    this.unitPrice = 0.0,
    double? totalCost,
    required this.dateOfAcquisition,
    this.modeOfAcquisition = 'Purchase',
    this.conditionStatus = 'Good',
    this.operationalStatus = 'In Storage',
    this.dimensions,
    this.color,
    this.model,
    this.others,
    this.remarks,
    this.rfidTag,
    this.photoUrl,
    this.localPhotoBytes,
    this.photoFileName,
    this.createdBy,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  })  : totalCost = totalCost ?? (quantity * unitPrice),
        createdAt = createdAt ?? DateTime.now(),
        updatedAt = updatedAt ?? DateTime.now();

  String get controlNumberPrefix =>
      '${locationAcronym.toUpperCase()}-${classificationAcronym.toUpperCase()} ${dateOfAcquisition.year}-';

  String get fullControlNumber => '$controlNumberPrefix${sequenceNumber.trim()}';

  String get formattedAcquisitionDate {
    return '${dateOfAcquisition.year}-${dateOfAcquisition.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${dateOfAcquisition.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  String get formattedUpdatedAt {
    return '${updatedAt.year}-${updatedAt.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${updatedAt.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')} '
        '${updatedAt.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${updatedAt.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  /// Evaluates fields to provide visual completion hints on the drafts page
  List<String> get missingChecklist {
    final List<String> missing = [];
    if (itemName.trim().isEmpty) missing.add('Item Name');
    if ((photoUrl == null || photoUrl!.trim().isEmpty) &&
        (localPhotoBytes == null || localPhotoBytes!.isEmpty)) {
      missing.add('Photograph');
    }
    if (unitPrice <= 0) missing.add('Acquisition Cost');
    if (dimensions == null || dimensions!.trim().isEmpty) missing.add('Dimensions');
    return missing;
  }

  bool get isReadyToRegister => missingChecklist.isEmpty;

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'draft_id': draftId,
      'item_name': itemName.trim().isEmpty ? null : itemName.trim(),
      'location_id': locationId,
      'location_acronym': locationAcronym.trim().toUpperCase(),
      'location_name': locationName,
      'classification_id': classificationId,
      'classification_acronym': classificationAcronym.trim().toUpperCase(),
      'classification_name': classificationName,
      'sequence_number': sequenceNumber.trim().isEmpty ? '001' : sequenceNumber.trim(),
      'quantity': quantity > 0 ? quantity : 1,
      'unit_price': unitPrice >= 0.0 ? unitPrice : 0.0,
      'total_cost': totalCost >= 0.0 ? totalCost : (quantity * unitPrice),
      'date_of_acquisition': formattedAcquisitionDate,
      'mode_of_acquisition': modeOfAcquisition,
      'condition_status': conditionStatus,
      'operational_status': operationalStatus,
      'dimensions': dimensions?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : dimensions!.trim(),
      'color': color?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : color!.trim(),
      'model': model?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : model!.trim(),
      'others': others?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : others!.trim(),
      'remarks': remarks?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : remarks!.trim(),
      'rfid_tag': rfidTag?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : rfidTag!.trim(),
      'photo_url': photoUrl?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : photoUrl!.trim(),
      'created_by': createdBy,
      'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'updated_at': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  factory AssetDraftModel.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    DateTime parseDate(dynamic value) {
      if (value == null) return DateTime.now();
      return DateTime.tryParse(value.toString()) ?? DateTime.now();
    }

    final acqDate = parseDate(map['date_of_acquisition']);
    final int qty = int.tryParse(map['quantity']?.toString() ?? '') ?? 1;
    final double uPrice = double.tryParse(map['unit_price']?.toString() ?? '') ?? 0.0;
    final double totCost = double.tryParse(map['total_cost']?.toString() ?? '') ?? (qty * uPrice);

    final rawStatus = map['operational_status']?.toString() ?? map['status']?.toString();

    return AssetDraftModel(
      draftId: map['draft_id']?.toString() ?? '',
      itemName: map['item_name']?.toString() ?? '',
      locationId: map['location_id']?.toString(),
      locationAcronym: map['location_acronym']?.toString() ?? 'C',
      locationName: map['location_name']?.toString(),
      classificationId: map['classification_id']?.toString(),
      classificationAcronym: map['classification_acronym']?.toString() ?? 'FF',
      classificationName: map['classification_name']?.toString(),
      sequenceNumber: map['sequence_number']?.toString() ?? '001',
      quantity: qty,
      unitPrice: uPrice,
      totalCost: totCost,
      dateOfAcquisition: acqDate,
      modeOfAcquisition: map['mode_of_acquisition']?.toString() ?? 'Purchase',
      conditionStatus: map['condition_status']?.toString() ?? 'Good',
      operationalStatus: AssetModel.normalizeStatus(rawStatus),
      dimensions: map['dimensions']?.toString(),
      color: map['color']?.toString(),
      model: map['model']?.toString(),
      others: map['others']?.toString(),
      remarks: map['remarks']?.toString(),
      rfidTag: map['rfid_tag']?.toString(),
      photoUrl: map['photo_url']?.toString(),
      createdBy: map['created_by']?.toString(),
      createdAt: parseDate(map['created_at']),
      updatedAt: parseDate(map['updated_at']),
    );
  }

  AssetDraftModel copyWith({
    String? draftId,
    String? itemName,
    String? locationId,
    String? locationAcronym,
    String? locationName,
    String? classificationId,
    String? classificationAcronym,
    String? classificationName,
    String? sequenceNumber,
    int? quantity,
    double? unitPrice,
    double? totalCost,
    DateTime? dateOfAcquisition,
    String? modeOfAcquisition,
    String? conditionStatus,
    String? operationalStatus,
    String? dimensions,
    String? color,
    String? model,
    String? others,
    String? remarks,
    String? rfidTag,
    String? photoUrl,
    Uint8List? localPhotoBytes,
    String? photoFileName,
    String? createdBy,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    final newQty = quantity ?? this.quantity;
    final newUnitPrice = unitPrice ?? this.unitPrice;
    final newTotalCost = totalCost ?? (newQty * newUnitPrice);

    return AssetDraftModel(
      draftId: draftId ?? this.draftId,
      itemName: itemName ?? this.itemName,
      locationId: locationId ?? this.locationId,
      locationAcronym: locationAcronym ?? this.locationAcronym,
      locationName: locationName ?? this.locationName,
      classificationId: classificationId ?? this.classificationId,
      classificationAcronym: classificationAcronym ?? this.classificationAcronym,
      classificationName: classificationName ?? this.classificationName,
      sequenceNumber: sequenceNumber ?? this.sequenceNumber,
      quantity: newQty,
      unitPrice: newUnitPrice,
      totalCost: newTotalCost,
      dateOfAcquisition: dateOfAcquisition ?? this.dateOfAcquisition,
      modeOfAcquisition: modeOfAcquisition ?? this.modeOfAcquisition,
      conditionStatus: conditionStatus ?? this.conditionStatus,
      operationalStatus: operationalStatus ?? this.operationalStatus,
      dimensions: dimensions ?? this.dimensions,
      color: color ?? this.color,
      model: model ?? this.model,
      others: others ?? this.others,
      remarks: remarks ?? this.remarks,
      rfidTag: rfidTag ?? this.rfidTag,
      photoUrl: photoUrl ?? this.photoUrl,
      localPhotoBytes: localPhotoBytes ?? this.localPhotoBytes,
      photoFileName: photoFileName ?? this.photoFileName,
      createdBy: createdBy ?? this.createdBy,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }
}

// FILE NAME: asset_model.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../../core/constants/colors.dart';

class AssetModel {
  final String assetId;
  final String controlNumber;
  final String itemName;
  final String? classificationId;
  final String classificationAcronym;
  final String? classificationName;
  final String? locationId;
  final String locationAcronym;
  final String? locationName;
  final String? dimensions;
  final String? color;
  final String? model;
  final String? others;
  final String? remarks;
  final String? photoUrl;
  final DateTime? dateOfAcquisition; // Nullable: allows recording year only
  final int acquisitionYear;
  final String modeOfAcquisition;

  // Pricing & Quantity structure
  final int quantity;
  final double unitPrice;
  final double totalCost;

  final String? rfidTag;
  final String qrCodeToken;

  /// Standardized Physical Condition:
  /// 'Excellent', 'Good', 'Fair', 'Poor', 'Damaged'
  final String conditionStatus;

  /// Standardized Availability:
  /// 'In Used', 'In Storage', 'Disposed'
  final String operationalStatus;

  final bool isArchived;
  final DateTime? archivedAt;
  final String? archivedBy;
  final String? archiveReason;

  final String? bulkBatchId;
  final int? itemSequenceInBatch;

  final DateTime? lastAuditedAt;
  final String? auditedBy;
  final String? createdBy;
  final DateTime registrationDate;
  final DateTime? createdAt;
  final DateTime? updatedAt;

  // ===========================================================================
  // Standard Constants & Canonical Lists
  // ===========================================================================

  static const List<String> standardConditions = [
    'Excellent',
    'Good',
    'Fair',
    'Poor',
    'Damaged',
  ];

  static const Map<String, String> conditionDescriptions = {
    'Excellent': 'Almost like new, no noticeable wear',
    'Good': 'Functional with minimal normal wear',
    'Fair': 'Functional with noticeable wear',
    'Poor': 'Functional but requires attention or repair',
    'Damaged': 'Physical damage or unserviceable',
  };

  /// Canonical Availability options across the entire system
  static const List<String> standardAvailability = [
    'In Used',
    'In Storage',
    'Disposed',
  ];

  static const Map<String, String> availabilityDescriptions = {
    'In Used': 'Currently deployed and actively utilized',
    'In Storage': 'Kept in storage, sacristy, or reserve stock',
    'Disposed': 'Officially decommissioned, retired, or disposed',
  };

  static const List<String> standardStatuses = standardAvailability;
  static const Map<String, String> statusDescriptions = availabilityDescriptions;

  AssetModel({
    required this.assetId,
    required this.controlNumber,
    required this.itemName,
    this.classificationId,
    required this.classificationAcronym,
    this.classificationName,
    this.locationId,
    required this.locationAcronym,
    this.locationName,
    this.dimensions,
    this.color,
    this.model,
    this.others,
    this.remarks,
    this.photoUrl,
    this.dateOfAcquisition,
    required this.acquisitionYear,
    this.modeOfAcquisition = 'Purchase',
    this.quantity = 1,
    this.unitPrice = 0.0,
    double? totalCost,
    this.rfidTag,
    required this.qrCodeToken,
    this.conditionStatus = 'Good',
    this.operationalStatus = 'In Storage',
    this.isArchived = false,
    this.archivedAt,
    this.archivedBy,
    this.archiveReason,
    this.bulkBatchId,
    this.itemSequenceInBatch,
    this.lastAuditedAt,
    this.auditedBy,
    this.createdBy,
    required this.registrationDate,
    this.createdAt,
    this.updatedAt,
  }) : totalCost = totalCost ?? (quantity * unitPrice);

  static String normalizeCondition(String? raw) {
    if (raw == null || raw.trim().isEmpty) return 'Good';
    final clean = raw.trim();

    for (final cond in standardConditions) {
      if (cond.toLowerCase() == clean.toLowerCase()) return cond;
    }

    final upper = clean.toUpperCase();
    if (upper.contains('EXCELLENT') || upper.contains('NEW')) return 'Excellent';
    if (upper.contains('GOOD') || upper.contains('VERIFIED')) return 'Good';
    if (upper.contains('FAIR')) return 'Fair';
    if (upper.contains('POOR') || upper.contains('REPAIR')) return 'Poor';
    if (upper.contains('DAMAGED') || upper.contains('UNSERVICEABLE') || upper.contains('UNUSABLE')) {
      return 'Damaged';
    }
    return 'Good';
  }

  static String normalizeStatus(String? raw) {
    if (raw == null || raw.trim().isEmpty) return 'In Storage';
    final clean = raw.trim();

    for (final st in standardAvailability) {
      if (st.toLowerCase() == clean.toLowerCase()) return st;
    }

    final lower = clean.toLowerCase();
    if (lower == 'in use' || lower == 'in used' || lower == 'active' || lower == 'deployed' || lower.contains('use')) {
      return 'In Used';
    }
    if (lower.contains('storage') || lower.contains('available') || lower.contains('reserved') || lower.contains('bodega') || lower.contains('sacristy')) {
      return 'In Storage';
    }
    if (lower.contains('disposed') || lower.contains('decommissioned') || lower.contains('retired') || lower.contains('condemned') || lower.contains('lost') || lower.contains('missing')) {
      return 'Disposed';
    }
    return 'In Storage';
  }

  /// Returns YYYY-MM-DD if full date is known, or just YYYY if month & day were optional.
  /// Never forces January 1!
  String get formattedAcquisitionDate {
    if (dateOfAcquisition == null) {
      return '$acquisitionYear';
    }
    return '${dateOfAcquisition!.year}-${dateOfAcquisition!.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${dateOfAcquisition!.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  String get formattedRegistrationDate {
    return '${registrationDate.year}-${registrationDate.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${registrationDate.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  String get formattedLastAuditedDate {
    if (lastAuditedAt == null) return 'Never Audited';
    return '${lastAuditedAt!.year}-${lastAuditedAt!.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${lastAuditedAt!.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  String get formattedUnitPrice => '₱ ${formatCurrency(unitPrice)}';
  String get formattedTotalCost => '₱ ${formatCurrency(totalCost)}';

  static String formatCurrency(double amount) {
    final parts = amount.toStringAsFixed(2).split('.');
    final integerPart = parts[0];
    final decimalPart = parts[1];

    final buffer = StringBuffer();
    for (int i = 0; i < integerPart.length; i++) {
      if (i > 0 && (integerPart.length - i) % 3 == 0) {
        buffer.write(',');
      }
      buffer.write(integerPart[i]);
    }
    return '${buffer.toString()}.$decimalPart';
  }

  String get inventorySection {
    if (unitPrice >= 10000.0) {
      return 'Section 1 — ₱10,000.00 and Above';
    } else {
      return 'Section 2 — Below ₱10,000.00';
    }
  }

  String get inventorySectionCode {
    return unitPrice >= 10000.0 ? 'Section 1' : 'Section 2';
  }

  bool get isEffectivelyArchived {
    if (isArchived) return true;
    final st = normalizeStatus(operationalStatus);
    return st == 'Disposed';
  }

  String get displayLocation {
    if (locationName != null && locationName!.isNotEmpty) {
      return '$locationName ($locationAcronym)';
    }
    return locationAcronym;
  }

  String get displayClassification {
    if (classificationName != null && classificationName!.isNotEmpty) {
      return '$classificationName ($classificationAcronym)';
    }
    return classificationAcronym;
  }

  Color get conditionColor {
    final normalized = normalizeCondition(conditionStatus);
    switch (normalized) {
      case 'Excellent':
        return const Color(0xFF0D9488);
      case 'Good':
        return ParishColors.oliveGreen;
      case 'Fair':
        return ParishColors.goldAccent;
      case 'Poor':
        return const Color(0xFFEA580C);
      case 'Damaged':
        return ParishColors.mercyRed;
      default:
        return const Color(0xFF64748B);
    }
  }

  Color get conditionSurfaceColor {
    final normalized = normalizeCondition(conditionStatus);
    switch (normalized) {
      case 'Excellent':
        return const Color(0xFFCCFBF1);
      case 'Good':
        return ParishColors.oliveGreenSurface;
      case 'Fair':
        return ParishColors.goldLight;
      case 'Poor':
        return const Color(0xFFFFEDD5);
      case 'Damaged':
      default:
        return const Color(0xFFF1F5F9);
    }
  }

  Color get statusColor {
    final normalized = normalizeStatus(operationalStatus);
    switch (normalized) {
      case 'In Used':
        return ParishColors.marianBlue;
      case 'In Storage':
        return ParishColors.oliveGreen;
      case 'Disposed':
      default:
        return ParishColors.mercyRed;
    }
  }

  Color get statusSurfaceColor {
    final normalized = normalizeStatus(operationalStatus);
    switch (normalized) {
      case 'In Used':
        return ParishColors.marianBlueSurface;
      case 'In Storage':
        return ParishColors.oliveGreenSurface;
      case 'Disposed':
      default:
        return ParishColors.mercyRedSurface;
    }
  }

  IconData get classificationIcon {
    switch (classificationAcronym.toUpperCase()) {
      case 'SI':
        return Icons.person_pin;
      case 'SV':
        return Icons.wine_bar;
      case 'MI':
        return Icons.piano;
      case 'LB':
        return Icons.menu_book;
      case 'FF':
        return Icons.chair_outlined;
      case 'OE':
        return Icons.computer;
      case 'EE':
        return Icons.electrical_services;
      case 'HT':
        return Icons.build;
      default:
        return Icons.inventory_2_outlined;
    }
  }

  factory AssetModel.fromMap(
      Map<String, dynamic> map, {
        List<AssetModel> children = const [],
      }) {
    DateTime parseDate(dynamic value) {
      if (value == null) return DateTime.now();
      return DateTime.tryParse(value.toString()) ?? DateTime.now();
    }

    DateTime? parseNullableDate(dynamic value) {
      if (value == null) return null;
      return DateTime.tryParse(value.toString());
    }

    final acqDate = parseNullableDate(map['date_of_acquisition'] ?? map['acquisition_date']);
    final int acqYear = int.tryParse(map['acquisition_year']?.toString() ?? '') ??
        (acqDate?.year ?? DateTime.now().year);

    String? classifName;
    if (map['asset_classifications'] is Map) {
      classifName =
          map['asset_classifications']['classification_name']?.toString();
    } else {
      classifName = map['category']?.toString();
    }

    String? locName;
    if (map['asset_locations'] is Map) {
      locName = map['asset_locations']['location_name']?.toString();
    } else {
      locName = map['storage_location']?.toString();
    }

    final int qty = int.tryParse(map['quantity']?.toString() ?? '') ?? 1;
    final double rawCost =
        double.tryParse(map['cost']?.toString() ?? '') ?? 0.0;
    final double uPrice = double.tryParse(
      map['unit_price']?.toString() ?? '',
    ) ??
        (rawCost > 0 ? (rawCost / (qty > 0 ? qty : 1)) : 0.0);
    final double totCost = double.tryParse(
      map['total_cost']?.toString() ?? '',
    ) ??
        (rawCost > 0 ? rawCost : (qty * uPrice));

    final rawCondition = map['condition_status']?.toString();
    final rawStatus = map['operational_status']?.toString() ??
        map['status']?.toString() ??
        'In Storage';

    return AssetModel(
      assetId: map['asset_id']?.toString() ??
          map['control_number']?.toString() ??
          '',
      controlNumber: map['control_number']?.toString() ?? '',
      itemName: map['item_name']?.toString() ?? '',
      classificationId: map['classification_id']?.toString(),
      classificationAcronym:
      map['classification_acronym']?.toString() ?? 'FF',
      classificationName: classifName,
      locationId: map['location_id']?.toString(),
      locationAcronym: map['location_acronym']?.toString() ?? 'C',
      locationName: locName,
      dimensions: map['dimensions']?.toString(),
      color: map['color']?.toString(),
      model: map['model']?.toString(),
      others: map['others']?.toString(),
      remarks: map['remarks']?.toString(),
      photoUrl: map['photo_url']?.toString(),
      dateOfAcquisition: acqDate,
      acquisitionYear: acqYear,
      modeOfAcquisition: map['mode_of_acquisition']?.toString() ??
          map['acquisition_mode']?.toString() ??
          'Purchase',
      quantity: qty > 0 ? qty : 1,
      unitPrice: uPrice >= 0 ? uPrice : 0.0,
      totalCost: totCost >= 0 ? totCost : 0.0,
      rfidTag: map['rfid_tag']?.toString(),
      qrCodeToken: map['qr_code_token']?.toString() ??
          map['control_number']?.toString() ??
          '',
      conditionStatus: normalizeCondition(rawCondition),
      operationalStatus: normalizeStatus(rawStatus),
      isArchived: map['is_archived'] == true,
      archivedAt: parseNullableDate(map['archived_at']),
      archivedBy: map['archived_by']?.toString(),
      archiveReason: map['archive_reason']?.toString(),
      bulkBatchId: map['bulk_batch_id']?.toString(),
      itemSequenceInBatch:
      int.tryParse(map['item_sequence_in_batch']?.toString() ?? ''),
      lastAuditedAt: parseNullableDate(map['last_audited_at']),
      auditedBy: map['audited_by']?.toString(),
      createdBy: map['created_by']?.toString(),
      registrationDate:
      parseDate(map['registration_date'] ?? map['created_at']),
      createdAt: parseNullableDate(map['created_at']),
      updatedAt: parseNullableDate(map['updated_at']),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    final String? dateString = dateOfAcquisition != null
        ? '${dateOfAcquisition!.year}-${dateOfAcquisition!.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${dateOfAcquisition!.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}'
        : null;

    return {
      'asset_id': assetId,
      'control_number': controlNumber,
      'item_name': itemName.trim(),
      'classification_id': classificationId,
      'classification_acronym': classificationAcronym.trim().toUpperCase(),
      'location_id': locationId,
      'location_acronym': locationAcronym.trim().toUpperCase(),
      'dimensions':
      dimensions?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : dimensions!.trim(),
      'color': color?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : color!.trim(),
      'model': model?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : model!.trim(),
      'others': others?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : others!.trim(),
      'remarks': remarks?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : remarks!.trim(),
      'photo_url': photoUrl?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : photoUrl!.trim(),
      'date_of_acquisition': dateString,
      'acquisition_year': acquisitionYear,
      'mode_of_acquisition': modeOfAcquisition,
      'quantity': quantity,
      'unit_price': unitPrice,
      'total_cost': totalCost,
      'cost': totalCost,
      'rfid_tag': rfidTag?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : rfidTag!.trim(),
      'qr_code_token': qrCodeToken,
      'condition_status': conditionStatus,
      'operational_status': operationalStatus,
      'is_archived': isArchived,
      'archived_at': archivedAt?.toIso8601String(),
      'archived_by': archivedBy,
      'archive_reason': archiveReason,
      'bulk_batch_id': bulkBatchId,
      'item_sequence_in_batch': itemSequenceInBatch,
      'last_audited_at': lastAuditedAt?.toIso8601String(),
      'audited_by': auditedBy,
      'created_by': createdBy,
      'registration_date': registrationDate.toIso8601String(),
      'category': classificationName ?? classificationAcronym,
      'storage_location': locationName ?? locationAcronym,
      'acquisition_date': dateString,
      'acquisition_mode': modeOfAcquisition,
      'description': remarks ?? others ?? itemName,
    };
  }
}

// =============================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// Model representing a Diocesan Location reference entry
class AssetLocationModel {
  final String locationId;
  final String acronym;
  final String locationName;
  final String? description;
  final bool isActive;
  final DateTime? createdAt;
  final DateTime? updatedAt;

  const AssetLocationModel({
    required this.locationId,
    required this.acronym,
    required this.locationName,
    this.description,
    this.isActive = true,
    this.createdAt,
    this.updatedAt,
  });

  String get displayName => '$locationName ($acronym)';

  factory AssetLocationModel.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return AssetLocationModel(
      locationId: map['location_id']?.toString() ?? '',
      acronym: map['acronym']?.toString().toUpperCase() ?? '',
      locationName: map['location_name']?.toString() ?? '',
      description: map['description']?.toString(),
      isActive: map['is_active'] ?? true,
      createdAt: map['created_at'] != null ? DateTime.tryParse(map['created_at'].toString()) : null,
      updatedAt: map['updated_at'] != null ? DateTime.tryParse(map['updated_at'].toString()) : null,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'location_id': locationId,
      'acronym': acronym.trim().toUpperCase(),
      'location_name': locationName.trim(),
      'description': description?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : description!.trim(),
      'is_active': isActive,
    };
  }

  AssetLocationModel copyWith({
    String? locationId,
    String? acronym,
    String? locationName,
    String? description,
    bool? isActive,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    return AssetLocationModel(
      locationId: locationId ?? this.locationId,
      acronym: acronym ?? this.acronym,
      locationName: locationName ?? this.locationName,
      description: description ?? this.description,
      isActive: isActive ?? this.isActive,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }
}

/// Model representing a Diocesan Asset Classification reference entry
class AssetClassificationModel {
  final String classificationId;
  final String acronym;
  final String classificationName;
  final String? description;
  final bool isActive;
  final DateTime? createdAt;
  final DateTime? updatedAt;

  const AssetClassificationModel({
    required this.classificationId,
    required this.acronym,
    required this.classificationName,
    this.description,
    this.isActive = true,
    this.createdAt,
    this.updatedAt,
  });

  String get displayName => '$classificationName ($acronym)';

  IconData get icon {
    switch (acronym.toUpperCase()) {
      case 'SI': // Sacred Image
        return Icons.person_pin;
      case 'SV': // Sacred Vessel
        return Icons.wine_bar;
      case 'MI': // Musical Instrument
        return Icons.piano;
      case 'LB': // Liturgical Book
        return Icons.menu_book;
      case 'FF': // Furniture and Fixtures
        return Icons.chair_outlined;
      case 'OE': // Office Equipment
        return Icons.computer;
      case 'EE': // Electrical Equipment
        return Icons.electrical_services;
      case 'HT': // Hand Tools
        return Icons.build;
      default:
        return Icons.inventory_2_outlined;
    }
  }

  factory AssetClassificationModel.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return AssetClassificationModel(
      classificationId: map['classification_id']?.toString() ?? '',
      acronym: map['acronym']?.toString().toUpperCase() ?? '',
      classificationName: map['classification_name']?.toString() ?? '',
      description: map['description']?.toString(),
      isActive: map['is_active'] ?? true,
      createdAt: map['created_at'] != null ? DateTime.tryParse(map['created_at'].toString()) : null,
      updatedAt: map['updated_at'] != null ? DateTime.tryParse(map['updated_at'].toString()) : null,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'classification_id': classificationId,
      'acronym': acronym.trim().toUpperCase(),
      'classification_name': classificationName.trim(),
      'description': description?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : description!.trim(),
      'is_active': isActive,
    };
  }

  AssetClassificationModel copyWith({
    String? classificationId,
    String? acronym,
    String? classificationName,
    String? description,
    bool? isActive,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    return AssetClassificationModel(
      classificationId: classificationId ?? this.classificationId,
      acronym: acronym ?? this.acronym,
      classificationName: classificationName ?? this.classificationName,
      description: description ?? this.description,
      isActive: isActive ?? this.isActive,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// FILE NAME: asset_inventory_view.dart

// =============================================================================

import 'dart:math';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../../core/constants/colors.dart';
import '../../../../core/services/records_sync_service.dart';
import '../models/asset_model.dart';
import '../models/asset_reference_models.dart';
import '../services/asset_draft_service.dart';
import '../services/asset_reference_service.dart';
import '../services/asset_service.dart';
import 'dialogs/asset_report_filter_dialog.dart';
import 'pages/asset_audit_scanner_page.dart';
import 'pages/asset_batch_print_page.dart';
import 'pages/asset_detail_page.dart';
import 'pages/asset_drafts_page.dart';
import 'pages/asset_references_page.dart';
import 'pages/register_asset_page.dart';
import 'widgets/asset_item_card.dart';

enum AssetViewMode { cards, table }

class AssetInventoryView extends StatefulWidget {
  const AssetInventoryView({super.key});

  @override
  State<AssetInventoryView> createState() => _AssetInventoryViewState();
}

class _AssetInventoryViewState extends State<AssetInventoryView>
    with TickerProviderStateMixin {
  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();
  final FocusNode _searchFocusNode = FocusNode();
  late TabController _sectionTabController;
  late AnimationController _skeletonAnimController;
  late Animation<double> _skeletonOpacityAnimation;

  List<AssetModel> _assets = [];
  List<AssetLocationModel> _locations = [];
  List<AssetClassificationModel> _classifications = [];

  bool _isLoading = true;
  String? _errorMessage;

  String _searchQuery = '';
  String _selectedCategory = 'All';
  String _selectedLocation = 'All';
  String _selectedCondition = 'All';
  String _selectedOperationalStatus = 'All';

  // Sorting State
  String _sortBy = 'Control Number';
  bool _sortAscending = true;

  final List<String> _sortOptions = [
    'Control Number',
    'Item Name',
    'Price / Unit',
    'Total Cost',
    'Acquisition Year',
    'Registration Date',
  ];

  // Standardized Canonical Condition Options
  final List<String> _conditionOptions = [
    'All',
    ...AssetModel.standardConditions,
  ];

  // Standardized Canonical Availability Options
  final List<String> _operationalStatusOptions = [
    'All',
    ...AssetModel.standardAvailability,
  ];

  AssetViewMode _viewMode = AssetViewMode.cards;

  // Standardized 1-Indexed Pagination State
  int _currentPage = 1;
  int _itemsPerPage = 10;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _sectionTabController = TabController(length: 4, vsync: this);
    _sectionTabController.addListener(() {
      if (!_sectionTabController.indexIsChanging) {
        setState(() {
          _currentPage = 1;
        });
      }
    });

    _skeletonAnimController = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 900),
    )..repeat(reverse: true);

    _skeletonOpacityAnimation = Tween<double>(begin: 0.35, end: 0.85).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(parent: _skeletonAnimController, curve: Curves.easeInOut),
    );

    RecordsSyncService.instance.checkConnectivity();
    RecordsSyncService.instance.updatePendingCount();
    AssetDraftService.initialize();
    _loadAllData();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    _searchFocusNode.dispose();
    _sectionTabController.dispose();
    _skeletonAnimController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _loadAllData() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _errorMessage = null;
    });

    try {
      final assetsFuture = AssetService.getAssets(includeArchived: true);
      final locsFuture = AssetReferenceService.getLocations(activeOnly: false);
      final classifsFuture =
      AssetReferenceService.getClassifications(activeOnly: false);

      final results =
      await Future.wait([assetsFuture, locsFuture, classifsFuture]);

      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _assets = results[0] as List<AssetModel>;
        _locations = results[1] as List<AssetLocationModel>;
        _classifications = results[2] as List<AssetClassificationModel>;
        _isLoading = false;
      });
      AssetDraftService.initialize();
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _errorMessage = e.toString().replaceFirst('Exception: ', '');
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  bool _isAssetArchived(AssetModel a) {
    return a.isEffectivelyArchived;
  }

  int _compareControlNumbers(String a, String b) {
    final aParts = a.split('-');
    final bParts = b.split('-');

    if (aParts.length >= 4 && bParts.length >= 4) {
      final prefixA = '${aParts[0]}-${aParts[1]}-${aParts[2]}';
      final prefixB = '${bParts[0]}-${bParts[1]}-${bParts[2]}';
      final prefixComp = prefixA.compareTo(prefixB);
      if (prefixComp != 0) return prefixComp;

      final seqA = int.tryParse(aParts[3]) ?? 0;
      final seqB = int.tryParse(bParts[3]) ?? 0;
      return seqA.compareTo(seqB);
    }
    return a.compareTo(b);
  }

  void _onSortChanged(String field) {
    setState(() {
      if (_sortBy == field) {
        _sortAscending = !_sortAscending;
      } else {
        _sortBy = field;
        _sortAscending = true;
      }
      _currentPage = 1;
    });
  }

  List<AssetModel> get _filteredAssets {
    var list = _assets;

    if (_sectionTabController.index == 3) {
      list = list.where((a) => _isAssetArchived(a)).toList();
    } else {
      list = list.where((a) => !_isAssetArchived(a)).toList();

      if (_sectionTabController.index == 1) {
        list = list.where((a) => a.unitPrice >= 10000.0).toList();
      } else if (_sectionTabController.index == 2) {
        list = list.where((a) => a.unitPrice < 10000.0).toList();
      }
    }

    if (_selectedCategory != 'All') {
      list = list
          .where((a) =>
      a.classificationAcronym.toUpperCase() ==
          _selectedCategory.toUpperCase())
          .toList();
    }

    if (_selectedLocation != 'All') {
      list = list
          .where((a) =>
      a.locationAcronym.toUpperCase() ==
          _selectedLocation.toUpperCase())
          .toList();
    }

    if (_selectedCondition != 'All') {
      list = list
          .where((a) =>
      AssetModel.normalizeCondition(a.conditionStatus).toLowerCase() ==
          _selectedCondition.toLowerCase())
          .toList();
    }

    if (_sectionTabController.index != 3 &&
        _selectedOperationalStatus != 'All') {
      list = list
          .where((a) =>
      AssetModel.normalizeStatus(a.operationalStatus).toLowerCase() ==
          _selectedOperationalStatus.toLowerCase())
          .toList();
    }

    if (_searchQuery.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      final q = _searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
      list = list.where((a) {
        return a.controlNumber.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
            a.itemName.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
            (a.model ?? '').toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
            (a.color ?? '').toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
            (a.rfidTag ?? '').toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
            a.displayLocation.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
            a.displayClassification.toLowerCase().contains(q);
      }).toList();
    }

    list.sort((a, b) {
      int comparison = 0;
      switch (_sortBy) {
        case 'Control Number':
          comparison =
              _compareControlNumbers(a.controlNumber, b.controlNumber);
          break;
        case 'Item Name':
          comparison =
              a.itemName.toLowerCase().compareTo(b.itemName.toLowerCase());
          break;
        case 'Price / Unit':
          comparison = a.unitPrice.compareTo(b.unitPrice);
          break;
        case 'Total Cost':
          comparison = a.totalCost.compareTo(b.totalCost);
          break;
        case 'Acquisition Year':
          comparison = a.acquisitionYear.compareTo(b.acquisitionYear);
          break;
        case 'Registration Date':
        default:
          comparison = a.registrationDate.compareTo(b.registrationDate);
          break;
      }
      return _sortAscending ? comparison : -comparison;
    });

    return list;
  }

  int get _activeCount => _assets.where((a) => !_isAssetArchived(a)).length;
  int get _archivedCount => _assets.where((a) => _isAssetArchived(a)).length;

  int get _section1Count =>
      _assets.where((a) => !_isAssetArchived(a) && a.unitPrice >= 10000.0).length;

  int get _section2Count =>
      _assets.where((a) => !_isAssetArchived(a) && a.unitPrice < 10000.0).length;

  int get _totalPages {
    final total = _filteredAssets.length;
    if (total == 0) return 1;
    return (total / _itemsPerPage).ceil();
  }

  int get _activeFilterCount {
    int count = 0;
    if (_selectedCategory != 'All') count++;
    if (_selectedLocation != 'All') count++;
    if (_selectedCondition != 'All') count++;
    if (_selectedOperationalStatus != 'All' &&
        _sectionTabController.index != 3) {
      count++;
    }
    if (_sortBy != 'Control Number' || !_sortAscending) count++;
    return count;
  }

  bool get _hasActiveFilters => _activeFilterCount > 0;

  void _resetFilters() {
    setState(() {
      _selectedCategory = 'All';
      _selectedLocation = 'All';
      _selectedCondition = 'All';
      _selectedOperationalStatus = 'All';
      _sortBy = 'Control Number';
      _sortAscending = true;
      _currentPage = 1;
    });
  }

  void _openRegisterAssetPage() {
    Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (_) => RegisterAssetPage(onAssetSaved: _loadAllData),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _openDraftsPage() {
    Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (_) => AssetDraftsPage(onAssetSaved: _loadAllData),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _openAuditScannerPage() {
    Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (_) => AssetAuditScannerPage(onScanCompleted: _loadAllData),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _openBatchPrintPage() {
    final list = _filteredAssets;
    if (list.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('No items match the current filter to print tags.'),
          backgroundColor: ParishColors.goldAccent,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (_) => AssetBatchPrintPage(availableAssets: list),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _openReportGeneratorDialog() {
    showAssetReportFilterModal(context, allAssets: _assets);
  }

  void _openManageReferencesPage() {
    Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (_) => AssetReferencesPage(onReferencesUpdated: _loadAllData),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _openAssetDetailPage(AssetModel asset, int indexInFiltered) {
    Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (_) => AssetDetailPage(
          assets: _filteredAssets,
          initialIndex: indexInFiltered,
          onAssetUpdated: _loadAllData,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _openFilterAndSortBottomSheet() {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: ParishColors.cardWhite,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (context, setSheetState) => Padding(
          padding: EdgeInsets.only(
            bottom: MediaQuery.of(ctx).viewInsets.bottom,
            left: 20,
            right: 20,
            top: 14,
          ),
          child: SafeArea(
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Center(
                    child: Container(
                      width: 36,
                      height: 4,
                      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: ParishColors.borderGrey,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        'Filter & Sort Inventory',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 17,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: ParishColors.textDark,
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (_hasActiveFilters)
                        TextButton(
                          onPressed: () {
                            _resetFilters();
                            setSheetState(() {});
                            Navigator.pop(ctx);
                          },
                          child: const Text(
                            'Reset All',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: ParishColors.mercyRed,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              fontSize: 13,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const Divider(height: 14),

                  // 1. Sort Order
                  Text(
                    'Sort Sequence',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 12.5,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: ParishColors.textDark,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                          value: _sortBy,
                          isExpanded: true,
                          decoration: InputDecoration(
                            contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                            border: OutlineInputBorder(
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                          ),
                          items: _sortOptions
                              .map((opt) => DropdownMenuItem(
                              value: opt,
                              child: Text(opt,
                                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13))))
                              .toList(),
                          onChanged: (val) {
                            if (val != null) {
                              setState(() => _sortBy = val);
                              setSheetState(() {});
                            }
                          },
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      IconButton(
                        style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                          backgroundColor: ParishColors.marianBlueSurface,
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                        ),
                        icon: Icon(
                          _sortAscending
                              ? Icons.arrow_upward
                              : Icons.arrow_downward,
                          size: 18,
                          color: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                        ),
                        onPressed: () {
                          setState(() => _sortAscending = !_sortAscending);
                          setSheetState(() {});
                        },
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),

                  // 2. Category Filter
                  Text(
                    'Classification Category',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 12.5,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: ParishColors.textDark,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                    value: _selectedCategory,
                    isExpanded: true,
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                      border: OutlineInputBorder(
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                    ),
                    items: [
                      const DropdownMenuItem(
                          value: 'All', child: Text('All Categories')),
                      ..._classifications.map((c) => DropdownMenuItem(
                          value: c.acronym,
                          child: Text('${c.classificationName} (${c.acronym})'))),
                    ],
                    onChanged: (val) {
                      if (val != null) {
                        setState(() {
                          _selectedCategory = val;
                          _currentPage = 1;
                        });
                        setSheetState(() {});
                      }
                    },
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),

                  // 3. Storage Location Filter
                  Text(
                    'Storage Location',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 12.5,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: ParishColors.textDark,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                    value: _selectedLocation,
                    isExpanded: true,
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                      border: OutlineInputBorder(
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                    ),
                    items: [
                      const DropdownMenuItem(
                          value: 'All', child: Text('All Locations')),
                      ..._locations.map((l) => DropdownMenuItem(
                          value: l.acronym,
                          child: Text('${l.locationName} (${l.acronym})'))),
                    ],
                    onChanged: (val) {
                      if (val != null) {
                        setState(() {
                          _selectedLocation = val;
                          _currentPage = 1;
                        });
                        setSheetState(() {});
                      }
                    },
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),

                  // 4. Item Condition Filter
                  Text(
                    'Item Physical Condition',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 12.5,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: ParishColors.textDark,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  SingleChildScrollView(
                    scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                    child: Row(
                      children: _conditionOptions.map((cond) {
                        final isSelected = _selectedCondition == cond;
                        return Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 6.0),
                          child: ChoiceChip(
                            label: Text(cond),
                            selected: isSelected,
                            selectedColor: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                            backgroundColor: ParishColors.backgroundLight,
                            labelStyle: TextStyle(
                              color: isSelected
                                  ? Colors.white
                                  : ParishColors.textDark,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              fontSize: 11.5,
                            ),
                            onSelected: (_) {
                              setState(() {
                                _selectedCondition = cond;
                                _currentPage = 1;
                              });
                              setSheetState(() {});
                            },
                          ),
                        );
                      }).toList(),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),

                  // 5. Item Availability Filter
                  if (_sectionTabController.index != 3) ...[
                    Text(
                      'Availability',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12.5,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: ParishColors.textDark,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 6),
                    SingleChildScrollView(
                      scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                      child: Row(
                        children: _operationalStatusOptions.map((st) {
                          final isSelected = _selectedOperationalStatus == st;
                          return Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 6.0),
                            child: ChoiceChip(
                              label: Text(st),
                              selected: isSelected,
                              selectedColor: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                              backgroundColor: ParishColors.backgroundLight,
                              labelStyle: TextStyle(
                                color: isSelected
                                    ? Colors.white
                                    : ParishColors.textDark,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                fontSize: 11.5,
                              ),
                              onSelected: (_) {
                                setState(() {
                                  _selectedOperationalStatus = st;
                                  _currentPage = 1;
                                });
                                setSheetState(() {});
                              },
                            ),
                          );
                        }).toList(),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 18),
                  ],

                  SizedBox(
                    width: double.infinity,
                    height: 46,
                    child: ElevatedButton(
                      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                        backgroundColor: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                      ),
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
                      child: const Text('Apply & Close',
                          style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHeroHeader(bool isMobile) {
    final fade = ParishColors.cardWhite;

    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
      child: Stack(
        children: [
          Positioned.fill(
            child: Image.asset(
              'images/jp2-bg.png',
              fit: BoxFit.cover,
              alignment: const Alignment(0.35, -0.55),
              errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) =>
                  Container(color: ParishColors.marianBlueSurface),
            ),
          ),
          Positioned.fill(
            child: DecoratedBox(
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                gradient: LinearGradient(
                  begin: Alignment.centerLeft,
                  end: Alignment.centerRight,
                  colors: [
                    fade.withOpacity(0.96),
                    fade.withOpacity(isMobile ? 0.88 : 0.80),
                    fade.withOpacity(isMobile ? 0.25 : 0.0),
                  ],
                  stops: [0.0, isMobile ? 0.62 : 0.45, isMobile ? 1.0 : 0.85],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Positioned.fill(
            child: DecoratedBox(
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                gradient: LinearGradient(
                  begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                  end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                  colors: [Colors.transparent, fade.withOpacity(0.55)],
                  stops: const [0.55, 1.0],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                isMobile ? 16 : 24, isMobile ? 16 : 22, isMobile ? 12 : 16, 14),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Container(
                            width: 56,
                            height: 3,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: ParishColors.goldAccent,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 12),
                          Text(
                            'Parish Asset Inventory',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontFamily: 'Georgia',
                              fontFamilyFallback: const ['Times New Roman', 'serif'],

                              height: 1.1,
                              color: ParishColors.textDark,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 6),
                          Text(
                            '$_activeCount active items tracked across parish locations and facilities',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: isMobile ? 12.0 : 14,
                              fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                              color: ParishColors.textDark.withOpacity(0.8),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],

                    _buildOverflowMenu(),

                SizedBox(height: isMobile ? 28 : 50),
                _buildSyncStatusBar(isMobile),
