                          Row(
                            children: [
                              Checkbox(
                                value: _isAllSelected,
                                activeColor: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                                visualDensity: VisualDensity.compact,
                                onChanged: _isBusy ? null : _toggleSelectAll,
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(width: 4),
                              Text(
                                isMobile
                                    ? (_isAllSelected
                                    ? 'Deselect'
                                    : 'All (${displayed.length})')
                                    : (_isAllSelected
                                    ? 'Deselect All'
                                    : 'Select All (${displayed.length})'),
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  fontSize: isMobile ? 12 : 13,
                                  color: textDark,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                          Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              horizontal: 10,
                              vertical: 4,
                            ),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: selectedCount > 0
                                  ? ParishColors.oliveGreenSurface
                                  : ParishColors.backgroundLight,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                              border: Border.all(
                                color: selectedCount > 0
                                    ? ParishColors.oliveGreen
                                    : borderGrey,
                              ),
                            ),
                            child: Text(
                              isMobile
                                  ? '$selectedCount / ${widget.availableAssets.length} Selected'
                                  : '$selectedCount of ${widget.availableAssets.length} Selected',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                fontSize: 11.5,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                color: selectedCount > 0
                                    ? ParishColors.oliveGreen
                                    : textMuted,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const Divider(height: 1),

                // Selectable List
                Expanded(
                  child: displayed.isEmpty
                      ? Center(
                    child: Text(
                      'No matching assets found.',
                      style: TextStyle(color: textMuted),
                    ),
                  )
                      : ListView.separated(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.all(isMobile ? 12 : 16),
                    itemCount: displayed.length,
                    separatorBuilder: (_, __) =>
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                      final a = displayed[index];
                      final isSelected = _selectedAssetIds.contains(
                        a.assetId,
                      );

                      return InkWell(
                        onTap: _isBusy
                            ? null
                            : () {
                          setState(() {
                            if (isSelected) {
                              _selectedAssetIds.remove(a.assetId);
                            } else {
                              _selectedAssetIds.add(a.assetId);
                            }
                          });
                        },
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: isSelected
                                ? ParishColors.marianBlueSurface
                                .withOpacity(0.4)
                                : cardWhite,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                            border: Border.all(
                              color: isSelected
                                  ? ParishColors.marianBlue
                                  : borderGrey,
                              width: isSelected ? 1.5 : 1.0,
                            ),
                          ),
                          child: Row(
                            children: [
                              Checkbox(
                                value: isSelected,
                                activeColor: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                                visualDensity: VisualDensity.compact,
                                onChanged: _isBusy
                                    ? null
                                    : (val) {
                                  setState(() {
                                    if (val == true) {
                                      _selectedAssetIds.add(
                                        a.assetId,
                                      );
                                    } else {
                                      _selectedAssetIds.remove(
                                        a.assetId,
                                      );
                                    }
                                  });
                                },
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(width: 8),
                              Expanded(
                                child: Column(
                                  crossAxisAlignment:
                                  CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                  children: [
                                    Row(
                                      children: [
                                        Text(
                                          a.controlNumber,
                                          style: const TextStyle(
                                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                            fontSize: 13,
                                            color:
                                            ParishColors.marianBlue,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                        if (a.quantity > 1) ...[
                                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                                          Container(
                                            padding:
                                            const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                              horizontal: 5,
                                              vertical: 1,
                                            ),
                                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                              color: ParishColors
                                                  .marianBlueSurface,
                                              borderRadius:
                                              BorderRadius.circular(
                                                3,
                                              ),
                                            ),
                                            child: Text(
                                              '${a.quantity} units',
                                              style: const TextStyle(
                                                fontSize: 9,
                                                fontWeight:
                                                FontWeight.bold,
                                                color: ParishColors
                                                    .marianBlue,
                                              ),
                                            ),
                                          ),
                                        ],
                                      ],
                                    ),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 2),
                                    Text(
                                      a.itemName,
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                        fontSize: 14,
                                        color: textDark,
                                      ),
                                      maxLines: 1,
                                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                    ),
                                    Text(
                                      '${a.displayClassification} • ${a.displayLocation}',
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                        fontSize: 11.5,
                                        color: textMuted,
                                      ),
                                      maxLines: 1,
                                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(width: 8),
                              Column(
                                crossAxisAlignment:
                                CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                                children: [
                                  Text(
                                    'Acq: ${a.acquisitionYear}',
                                    style: TextStyle(
                                      fontSize: 11,
                                      color: textMuted,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  Text(
                                    a.formattedUnitPrice,
                                    style: const TextStyle(
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                      color: ParishColors.oliveGreen,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// FILE NAME: asset_camera_page.dart
// FILE PATH: lib/features/asset_inventory/presentation/pages/asset_camera_page.dart
// =============================================================================

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/asset_inventory/presentation/pages/asset_camera_page.dart
// =============================================================================

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io' show Platform;
import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'dart:ui' as ui;
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart' show kIsWeb;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';
import 'package:mobile_scanner/mobile_scanner.dart';
import '../../../../core/constants/colors.dart';
import '../../utils/asset_image_watermark_util.dart';
import '../../utils/web_camera_helper.dart';

enum AssetCameraRatio {
  landscape3R, // 5" x 3.5" (10:7 ratio ≈ 1.4286)
  portrait3R, // 3.5" x 5" (7:10 ratio = 0.7000)
}

class AssetCameraPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String assetIdentifier;

  const AssetCameraPage({
    super.key,
    required this.assetIdentifier,
  });

  @override
  State<AssetCameraPage> createState() => _AssetCameraPageState();
}

class _AssetCameraPageState extends State<AssetCameraPage> {
  MobileScannerController? _scannerController;
  AssetCameraRatio _selectedRatio = AssetCameraRatio.landscape3R;
  bool _isTorchOn = false;
  bool _isProcessing = false;
  bool _cameraPermissionDenied = false;

  bool get _isLiveCameraSupported {
    if (kIsWeb) return true;
    try {
      return Platform.isAndroid || Platform.isIOS || Platform.isMacOS;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  double get _targetAspectRatio {
    switch (_selectedRatio) {
      case AssetCameraRatio.landscape3R:
        return 10.0 / 7.0; // 5 x 3.5 inches
      case AssetCameraRatio.portrait3R:
        return 7.0 / 10.0; // 3.5 x 5 inches
    }
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _initScanner();
  }

  void _initScanner() {
    if (!_isLiveCameraSupported) return;
    try {
      _scannerController = MobileScannerController(
        detectionSpeed: DetectionSpeed.normal,
        facing: CameraFacing.back,
        torchEnabled: false,
        autoStart: true,
      );
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error creating camera scanner controller: $e');
    }
  }

  @override
  void deactivate() {
    _safeStopCamera();
    super.deactivate();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _safeStopCamera();
    try {
      _scannerController?.dispose();
    } catch (_) {}
    _scannerController = null;
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _safeStopCamera() async {
    try {
      if (_scannerController != null) {
        await _scannerController!.stop();
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    WebCameraHelper.releaseCamera();
  }

  Future<void> _handleClose() async {
    await _safeStopCamera();
    if (mounted) {
      Navigator.pop(context, null);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _toggleTorch() async {
    if (_scannerController == null) return;
    try {
      await _scannerController!.toggleTorch();
      setState(() => _isTorchOn = !_isTorchOn);
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> _switchCamera() async {
    if (_scannerController == null) return;
    try {
      await _scannerController!.switchCamera();
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // Frame Capture, 3R Aspect Ratio Cropping, and Watermarking
  // ===========================================================================

  Future<void> _capturePhoto() async {
    if (_isProcessing) return;
    setState(() => _isProcessing = true);

    try {
      Uint8List? rawBytes;

      if (kIsWeb) {
        rawBytes = WebCameraHelper.captureFrame();
      } else {
        // Native mobile fallback if video canvas element is not in DOM
        await _safeStopCamera();
        final picker = ImagePicker();
        final photo = await picker.pickImage(
          source: ImageSource.camera,
          imageQuality: 95,
        );
        if (photo != null) {
          rawBytes = await photo.readAsBytes();
        }
      }

      if (rawBytes == null || rawBytes.isEmpty) {
        setState(() => _isProcessing = false);
        if (mounted) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            const SnackBar(
              content: Text(
                  'Could not capture image from stream. Please ensure camera is active.'),
              backgroundColor: ParishColors.goldAccent,
            ),
          );
        }
        return;
      }

      // Crop the snapshot to match the selected 3R aspect ratio
      final croppedBytes = await _cropImageTo3RRatio(rawBytes, _targetAspectRatio);

      // Apply diocesan diagonal tiled security watermark
      final watermarkedBytes = await AssetImageWatermarkUtil.applySecurityWatermark(
        rawImageBytes: croppedBytes,
        assetIdentifier: widget.assetIdentifier,
        captureTime: DateTime.now(),
      );

      await _safeStopCamera();

      if (mounted) {
        Navigator.pop(context, watermarkedBytes);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error capturing 3R photo: $e');
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isProcessing = false);
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('Capture error: $e'),
            backgroundColor: ParishColors.mercyRed,
          ),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  /// Crops any raw image to the exact 3R aspect ratio (10:7 or 7:10)
  Future<Uint8List> _cropImageTo3RRatio(
      Uint8List rawBytes,
      double targetRatio,
      ) async {
    final Completer<ui.Image> completer = Completer();
    ui.decodeImageFromList(rawBytes, (ui.Image img) {
      completer.complete(img);
    });

    final ui.Image image = await completer.future;
    final double imgW = image.width.toDouble();
    final double imgH = image.height.toDouble();
    final double currentRatio = imgW / imgH;

    double cropW = imgW;
    double cropH = imgH;

    if (currentRatio > targetRatio) {
      // Source image is wider than 3R target -> trim horizontal margins
      cropW = imgH * targetRatio;
    } else {
      // Source image is taller than 3R target -> trim vertical margins
      cropH = imgW / targetRatio;
    }

    final double cropX = (imgW - cropW) / 2.0;
    final double cropY = (imgH - cropH) / 2.0;

    final ui.PictureRecorder recorder = ui.PictureRecorder();
    final Canvas canvas = Canvas(
      recorder,
      Rect.fromLTWH(0, 0, cropW, cropH),
    );

    canvas.drawImageRect(
      image,
      Rect.fromLTWH(cropX, cropY, cropW, cropH),
      Rect.fromLTWH(0, 0, cropW, cropH),
      Paint()..filterQuality = FilterQuality.high,
    );

    final ui.Picture picture = recorder.endRecording();
    final ui.Image croppedImage = await picture.toImage(
      cropW.toInt(),
      cropH.toInt(),
    );

    final ByteData? byteData = await croppedImage.toByteData(
      format: ui.ImageByteFormat.png,
    );

    return byteData != null ? byteData.buffer.asUint8List() : rawBytes;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) {
        final double screenWidth = constraints.maxWidth;
        final double screenHeight = constraints.maxHeight;
        final bool isMobile = screenWidth < 650;

        return PopScope(
          canPop: false,
          onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, result) async {
            if (didPop) return;
            await _handleClose();
          },
          child: Scaffold(
            backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
            body: SafeArea(
              child: Stack(
                children: [
                  // 1. Live Camera Viewfinder
                  Positioned.fill(
                    child: _isLiveCameraSupported &&
                        _scannerController != null &&
                        !_cameraPermissionDenied
                        ? MobileScanner(
                      controller: _scannerController!,
                      onDetect: (_) {},
                      errorBuilder: (ctx, err) {
                        return Center(
                          child: Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
                            child: Text(
                              'Camera error: ${err.errorDetails?.message ?? "Permission required"}',
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                color: Colors.white70,
                                fontSize: 13,
                              ),
                              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                            ),
                          ),
                        );
                      },
                    )
                        : const Center(
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        strokeWidth: 2.5,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),

                  // 2. Translucent Mask with 3R Cutout Viewport
                  Positioned.fill(
                    child: _build3RMaskOverlay(
                      screenWidth: screenWidth,
                      screenHeight: screenHeight,
                      isMobile: isMobile,
                    ),
                  ),

                  // 3. Top Header Bar (Close Button, Title & Identifier)
                  Positioned(
                    top: 12,
                    left: 12,
                    right: 12,
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        IconButton(
                          style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                            backgroundColor: Colors.black54,
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                          ),
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.white, size: 22),
                          tooltip: 'Close Camera',
                          onPressed: _isProcessing ? null : _handleClose,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 10),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                            children: [
                              const Text(
                                '3R Photo Capture',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: Colors.white,
                                  fontSize: 15,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                ),
                              ),
                              Text(
                                widget.assetIdentifier,
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  color: Color(0xFFD49B18),
                                  fontSize: 11,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                ),
                                maxLines: 1,
                                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                        IconButton(
                          style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                            backgroundColor: Colors.black54,
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                          ),
                          icon: Icon(
                            _isTorchOn ? Icons.flash_on : Icons.flash_off,
                            color: Colors.white,
                            size: 20,
                          ),
                          tooltip: 'Flash',
                          onPressed: _isProcessing ? null : _toggleTorch,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 6),
                        IconButton(
                          style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                            backgroundColor: Colors.black54,
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                          ),
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.cameraswitch, color: Colors.white, size: 20),
                          tooltip: 'Switch Camera',
                          onPressed: _isProcessing ? null : _switchCamera,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),

                  // 4. Bottom Controls: 3R Aspect Ratio Selector & Shutter Button
                  Positioned(
                    bottom: 16,
                    left: 16,
                    right: 16,
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        // 3R Ratio Segmented Selector
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.black87,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                            border: Border.all(color: Colors.white24),
                          ),
                          child: Row(
                            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                            children: [
                              _buildRatioTab(
                                label: '3R Landscape (5 × 3.5)',
                                icon: Icons.crop_landscape,
                                isSelected:
                                _selectedRatio == AssetCameraRatio.landscape3R,
                                onTap: () => setState(() =>
                                _selectedRatio = AssetCameraRatio.landscape3R),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(width: 4),
                              _buildRatioTab(
                                label: '3R Portrait (3.5 × 5)',
                                icon: Icons.crop_portrait,
                                isSelected:
                                _selectedRatio == AssetCameraRatio.portrait3R,
                                onTap: () => setState(() =>
                                _selectedRatio = AssetCameraRatio.portrait3R),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 18),

                        // Shutter Button Row
                        Row(
                          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                          children: [
                            GestureDetector(
                              onTap: _isProcessing ? null : _capturePhoto,
                              child: Container(
                                width: 72,
                                height: 72,
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                  border: Border.all(
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                    width: 3.5,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                child: Container(
                                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                    color: _isProcessing
                                        ? Colors.white54
                                        : ParishColors.goldAccent,
                                  ),
                                  child: _isProcessing
                                      ? const Center(
                                    child: SizedBox(
                                      width: 26,
                                      height: 26,
                                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                                        color: Colors.white,
                                        strokeWidth: 2.5,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  )
                                      : const Icon(
                                    Icons.camera_alt,
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                    size: 30,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 6),
                        Text(
                          _selectedRatio == AssetCameraRatio.landscape3R
                              ? '3R Landscape • Standard 10:7 print ratio'
                              : '3R Portrait • Standard 7:10 print ratio',
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            color: Colors.white70,
                            fontSize: 11,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),

                  // 5. Processing / Watermarking Overlay
                  if (_isProcessing)
                    Container(
                      color: Colors.black54,
                      child: const Center(
                        child: Column(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            CircularProgressIndicator(
                              color: Color(0xFFD49B18),
                              strokeWidth: 3,
                            ),
                            SizedBox(height: 14),
                            Text(
                              'Cropping to 3R & embedding watermark...',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: Colors.white,
                                fontSize: 13.5,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // 3R Viewfinder Mask Overlay Painter
  // ===========================================================================

  Widget _build3RMaskOverlay({
    required double screenWidth,
    required double screenHeight,
    required bool isMobile,
  }) {
    // Available bounding area for the viewport
    final double maxWidth = screenWidth - 36;
    final double maxHeight = screenHeight - 240;

    double boxWidth;
    double boxHeight;

    if (_selectedRatio == AssetCameraRatio.landscape3R) {
      boxWidth = maxWidth.clamp(280.0, 720.0);
      boxHeight = boxWidth / (10.0 / 7.0);
      if (boxHeight > maxHeight) {
        boxHeight = maxHeight;
        boxWidth = boxHeight * (10.0 / 7.0);
      }
    } else {
      boxHeight = maxHeight.clamp(320.0, 620.0);
      boxWidth = boxHeight * (7.0 / 10.0);
      if (boxWidth > maxWidth) {
        boxWidth = maxWidth;
        boxHeight = boxWidth / (7.0 / 10.0);
      }
    }

    return Center(
      child: Container(
        width: boxWidth,
        height: boxHeight,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          border: Border.all(
            color: const Color(0xFFD49B18),
            width: 2.5,
          ),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.55),
              spreadRadius: 2000,
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: Stack(
          children: [
            // Rule of thirds grid lines
            Column(
              children: [
                const Spacer(flex: 1),
                Divider(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2), height: 1),
                const Spacer(flex: 1),
                Divider(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2), height: 1),
                const Spacer(flex: 1),
              ],
            ),
            Row(
              children: [
                const Spacer(flex: 1),
                VerticalDivider(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2), width: 1),
                const Spacer(flex: 1),
                VerticalDivider(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2), width: 1),
                const Spacer(flex: 1),
              ],
            ),
            // Corner target indicators
            Align(
              alignment: Alignment.topLeft,
              child: _buildCornerWidget(),
            ),
            Align(
              alignment: Alignment.topRight,
              child: Transform.rotate(angle: pi / 2, child: _buildCornerWidget()),
            ),
            Align(
              alignment: Alignment.bottomLeft,
              child: Transform.rotate(angle: -pi / 2, child: _buildCornerWidget()),
            ),
            Align(
              alignment: Alignment.bottomRight,
              child: Transform.rotate(angle: pi, child: _buildCornerWidget()),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCornerWidget() {
    return Container(
      width: 18,
      height: 18,
      margin: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        border: Border(
          top: BorderSide(color: Color(0xFFD49B18), width: 3),
          left: BorderSide(color: Color(0xFFD49B18), width: 3),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRatioTab({
    required String label,
    required IconData icon,
    required bool isSelected,
    required VoidCallback onTap,
  }) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 7),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: isSelected ? const Color(0xFFD49B18) : Colors.transparent,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Icon(
              icon,
              size: 15,
              color: isSelected ? Colors.white : Colors.white70,
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 11.5,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                color: isSelected ? Colors.white : Colors.white70,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'package:file_picker/file_picker.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import '../../../../core/constants/colors.dart';
import '../../../auth/services/auth_service.dart';
import '../../models/asset_audit_log_model.dart';
import '../../models/asset_model.dart';
import '../../models/asset_reference_models.dart';
import '../../services/asset_label_pdf_service.dart';
import '../../services/asset_reference_service.dart';
import '../../services/asset_service.dart';
import '../../utils/asset_image_watermark_util.dart';
import 'asset_camera_page.dart';

class ThousandsSeparatorCurrencyFormatter extends TextInputFormatter {
  @override
  TextEditingValue formatEditUpdate(
      TextEditingValue oldValue,
      TextEditingValue newValue,
      ) {
    if (newValue.text.isEmpty) {
      return newValue.copyWith(text: '');
    }

    final clean = newValue.text.replaceAll(',', '');
    if (!RegExp(r'^\d*(\.\d{0,2})?$').hasMatch(clean)) {
      return oldValue;
    }

    final parts = clean.split('.');
    final integerPart = parts[0];
    final decimalPart = parts.length > 1 ? parts[1] : null;

    final buffer = StringBuffer();
    for (int i = 0; i < integerPart.length; i++) {
      if (i > 0 && (integerPart.length - i) % 3 == 0) {
        buffer.write(',');
      }
      buffer.write(integerPart[i]);
    }

    String formatted = buffer.toString();
    if (decimalPart != null) {
      formatted += '.$decimalPart';
    }

    return TextEditingValue(
      text: formatted,
      selection: TextSelection.collapsed(offset: formatted.length),
    );
  }
}

class AssetDetailPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<AssetModel> assets;
  final int initialIndex;
  final VoidCallback? onAssetUpdated;

  const AssetDetailPage({
    super.key,
    required this.assets,
    required this.initialIndex,
    this.onAssetUpdated,
  });

  @override
  State<AssetDetailPage> createState() => _AssetDetailPageState();
}

class _AssetDetailPageState extends State<AssetDetailPage> {
  late List<AssetModel> _assetList;
  late int _currentIndex;

  AssetModel get _currentAsset => _assetList[_currentIndex];

  bool _isLoadingHistory = false;
  List<AssetAuditLogModel> _auditHistory = [];

  bool _isEditing = false;
  bool _isUpdating = false;
  bool _isProcessingImage = false;

  Uint8List? _newWatermarkedPhotoBytes;
  String? _newPhotoFileName;

  late TextEditingController _nameController;
  late TextEditingController _quantityController;
  late TextEditingController _unitPriceController;
  late TextEditingController _dimensionsController;
  late TextEditingController _colorController;
  late TextEditingController _modelController;
  late TextEditingController _othersController;
  late TextEditingController _remarksController;
  late TextEditingController _rfidController;

  List<AssetLocationModel> _locations = [];
  List<AssetClassificationModel> _classifications = [];
  AssetLocationModel? _selectedLocation;
  AssetClassificationModel? _selectedClassification;

  // Separated inline date mechanism (Month & Day are optional, no future dates allowed)
  late int _selectedYear;
  int? _selectedMonth; // null = optional/unspecified, NEVER forced to January
  int? _selectedDay; // null = optional/unspecified, NEVER forced to 1st

  late String _conditionStatus;
  late String _operationalStatus;
  late String _modeOfAcquisition;

  final List<String> _acquisitionModes = [
    'Purchase',
    'Donation',
    'Transfer',
    'Diocesan Grant',
    'Inherited / Legacy',
    'Found / Recovered',
  ];

  final List<String> _conditionOptions = AssetModel.standardConditions;
  final List<String> _operationalStatusOptions = AssetModel.standardAvailability;

  static const List<String> _monthNames = [
    'January',
    'February',
    'March',
    'April',
    'May',
    'June',
    'July',
    'August',
    'September',
    'October',
    'November',
    'December',
  ];

  bool get _hasPrevious => _currentIndex > 0;
  bool get _hasNext => _currentIndex < _assetList.length - 1;

  bool get _canModify {
    final user = AuthService.currentUser;
    if (user == null) return true;
    final role = user.userRole
        .toLowerCase()
        .replaceAll('_', '')
        .replaceAll(' ', '')
        .trim();
    return role == 'superadmin' ||
        role == 'admin' ||
        role == 'parishpriest' ||
        role == 'secretary' ||
        role == 'encoder' ||
        role.isEmpty;
  }

  bool get _canArchive {
    final user = AuthService.currentUser;
    if (user == null) return true;
    final role = user.userRole
        .toLowerCase()
        .replaceAll('_', '')
        .replaceAll(' ', '')
        .trim();
    return role == 'superadmin' ||
        role == 'admin' ||
        role == 'parishpriest' ||
        role.isEmpty;
  }

  int get _editQuantity {
    final q = int.tryParse(_quantityController.text.trim());
    return (q != null && q > 0) ? q : 1;
  }

  double get _editUnitPrice {
    final clean = _unitPriceController.text.replaceAll(',', '').trim();
    if (clean.isEmpty) return 0.0;
    return double.tryParse(clean) ?? 0.0;
  }

  double get _editCalculatedTotalCost => _editQuantity * _editUnitPrice;

  String get _editDeterminedSection {
    return _editUnitPrice >= 10000.0
        ? 'Section 1 — ₱10,000.00 and Above'
        : 'Section 2 — Below ₱10,000.00';
  }

  /// Returns null if month or day was left optional, completely preventing false "January 1" outputs
  DateTime? get _effectiveDateOfAcquisition {
    if (_selectedMonth != null && _selectedDay != null) {
      return DateTime(_selectedYear, _selectedMonth!, _selectedDay!);
    }
    return null;
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _assetList = List.from(widget.assets);
    _currentIndex = widget.initialIndex
        .clamp(0, _assetList.isEmpty ? 0 : _assetList.length - 1);
    _initializeCurrentAsset();
    _loadReferenceOptions();
  }

  void _initializeCurrentAsset() {
    final asset = _currentAsset;
    final now = DateTime.now();

    _conditionStatus = AssetModel.normalizeCondition(asset.conditionStatus);
    _operationalStatus = AssetModel.normalizeStatus(asset.operationalStatus);
    _modeOfAcquisition = asset.modeOfAcquisition;

    final initialDate = asset.dateOfAcquisition;
    final cappedYear = (initialDate != null && initialDate.year > now.year)
        ? now.year
        : (asset.acquisitionYear > now.year ? now.year : asset.acquisitionYear);
    _selectedYear = cappedYear;

    // Only populate month/day if explicitly recorded; otherwise keep null
    if (initialDate != null) {
      if (_selectedYear == now.year && initialDate.month > now.month) {
        _selectedMonth = now.month;
      } else {
        _selectedMonth = initialDate.month;
      }

      if (_selectedYear == now.year &&
          _selectedMonth == now.month &&
          initialDate.day > now.day) {
        _selectedDay = now.day;
      } else {
        _selectedDay = initialDate.day;
      }
    } else {
      _selectedMonth = null;
      _selectedDay = null;
    }

    _nameController = TextEditingController(text: asset.itemName);
    _quantityController = TextEditingController(text: '${asset.quantity}');
    _unitPriceController = TextEditingController(
      text:
      asset.unitPrice > 0 ? AssetModel.formatCurrency(asset.unitPrice) : '',
    );
    _dimensionsController = TextEditingController(text: asset.dimensions ?? '');
    _colorController = TextEditingController(text: asset.color ?? '');
    _modelController = TextEditingController(text: asset.model ?? '');
    _othersController = TextEditingController(text: asset.others ?? '');
    _remarksController = TextEditingController(text: asset.remarks ?? '');
    _rfidController = TextEditingController(text: asset.rfidTag ?? '');

    _quantityController.addListener(() => setState(() {}));
    _unitPriceController.addListener(() => setState(() {}));

    _newWatermarkedPhotoBytes = null;
    _newPhotoFileName = null;
    _isEditing = false;

    _loadAuditHistory();
  }

  void _disposeControllers() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _quantityController.dispose();
    _unitPriceController.dispose();
    _dimensionsController.dispose();
    _colorController.dispose();
    _modelController.dispose();
    _othersController.dispose();
    _remarksController.dispose();
    _rfidController.dispose();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _disposeControllers();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _navigateToPrevious() {
    if (!_hasPrevious) return;
    _disposeControllers();
    setState(() {
      _currentIndex--;
      _initializeCurrentAsset();
    });
  }

  void _navigateToNext() {
    if (!_hasNext) return;
    _disposeControllers();
    setState(() {
      _currentIndex++;
      _initializeCurrentAsset();
    });
  }

  Future<void> _loadReferenceOptions() async {
    try {
      final locs = await AssetReferenceService.getLocations(activeOnly: false);
      final classifs =
      await AssetReferenceService.getClassifications(activeOnly: false);
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _locations = locs;
        _classifications = classifs;
        _selectedLocation = locs.firstWhere(
              (l) =>
          l.acronym.toUpperCase() ==
              _currentAsset.locationAcronym.toUpperCase(),
          orElse: () => locs.first,
        );
        _selectedClassification = classifs.firstWhere(
              (c) =>
          c.acronym.toUpperCase() ==
              _currentAsset.classificationAcronym.toUpperCase(),
          orElse: () => classifs.first,
        );
      });
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> _loadAuditHistory() async {
    setState(() => _isLoadingHistory = true);
    try {
      final history =
      await AssetService.getAssetAuditHistory(_currentAsset.assetId);
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _auditHistory = history;
        _isLoadingHistory = false;
      });
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoadingHistory = false);
    }
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // Dedicated 3R Camera Launch (Landscape & Portrait Framing)
  // ===========================================================================

  Future<void> _openDedicated3RCameraPage() async {
    final idLabel =
        '${_currentAsset.controlNumber} • ${_nameController.text.trim()}';

    final Uint8List? capturedWatermarkedBytes =
    await Navigator.push<Uint8List>(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (_) => AssetCameraPage(assetIdentifier: idLabel),
      ),
    );

    if (capturedWatermarkedBytes != null &&
        capturedWatermarkedBytes.isNotEmpty) {
      setState(() {
        _newWatermarkedPhotoBytes = capturedWatermarkedBytes;
        _newPhotoFileName =
        'photo_3R_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}.png';
      });

      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(
            content: Text(
              '3R photographic evidence captured and security watermark applied successfully.',
            ),
            backgroundColor: ParishColors.oliveGreen,
            duration: Duration(seconds: 3),
          ),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _pickImageFromDevice() async {
    try {
      final dynamic result = await FilePicker.pickFiles(
        type: FileType.custom,
        allowedExtensions: ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'webp'],
      );

      if (result != null) {
        dynamic file;
        if (result is List && result.isNotEmpty) {
          file = result.first;
        } else {
          try {
            final files = (result as dynamic).files;
            if (files != null && files.isNotEmpty) file = files.first;
          } catch (_) {
            file = result;
          }
        }

        if (file != null) {
          Uint8List? rawBytes;
          try {
            rawBytes = await (file as dynamic).readAsBytes();
          } catch (_) {
            try {
              rawBytes = (file as dynamic).bytes;
            } catch (_) {}
          }

          String? name;
          try {
            name = (file as dynamic).name;
          } catch (_) {}

          if (rawBytes != null) {
            await _processAndWatermarkNewImage(
              rawBytes,
              name ?? 'asset_photo.jpg',
            );
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error uploading image: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _processAndWatermarkNewImage(
      Uint8List rawBytes,
      String filename,
      ) async {
    setState(() => _isProcessingImage = true);

    try {
      final idLabel =
          '${_currentAsset.controlNumber} • ${_nameController.text.trim()}';

      final watermarked = await AssetImageWatermarkUtil.applySecurityWatermark(
        rawImageBytes: rawBytes,
        assetIdentifier: idLabel,
        captureTime: DateTime.now(),
      );

      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _newWatermarkedPhotoBytes = watermarked;
        _newPhotoFileName = filename;
        _isProcessingImage = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _newWatermarkedPhotoBytes = rawBytes;
        _newPhotoFileName = filename;
        _isProcessingImage = false;
      });
    }
  }

  void _openInteractivePreview(ImageProvider imageProvider) {
    AssetImageWatermarkUtil.showImagePreviewModal(
      context,
      imageProvider: imageProvider,
      title: _currentAsset.itemName,
      controlNumber: _currentAsset.controlNumber,
    );
  }

  Future<void> _saveAssetUpdates() async {
    // Strict validation: verify date cannot be in the future
    final now = DateTime.now();
    if (_selectedYear > now.year ||
        (_selectedYear == now.year &&
            _selectedMonth != null &&
            _selectedMonth! > now.month) ||
        (_selectedYear == now.year &&
            _selectedMonth != null &&
            _selectedMonth == now.month &&
            _selectedDay != null &&
            _selectedDay! > now.day)) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Date of acquisition cannot be set to a future date.'),
          backgroundColor: ParishColors.mercyRed,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isUpdating = true);
    try {
      String? photoUrlToSave = _currentAsset.photoUrl;

      if (_newWatermarkedPhotoBytes != null && _newPhotoFileName != null) {
        final uploaded = await AssetService.uploadAssetPhoto(
          assetId: _currentAsset.assetId,
          fileBytes: _newWatermarkedPhotoBytes!,
          fileName: _newPhotoFileName!,
        );
        if (uploaded != null) {
          photoUrlToSave = uploaded;
        }
      }

      final updated = await AssetService.updateAsset(
        assetId: _currentAsset.assetId,
        itemName: _nameController.text.trim(),
        classificationAcronym: _currentAsset.classificationAcronym,
        locationAcronym: _currentAsset.locationAcronym,
        locationName: _currentAsset.locationName,
        dimensions: _dimensionsController.text.trim(),
        color: _colorController.text.trim(),
        model: _modelController.text.trim(),
        others: _othersController.text.trim(),
        remarks: _remarksController.text.trim(),
        photoUrl: photoUrlToSave,
        dateOfAcquisition: _effectiveDateOfAcquisition,
        acquisitionYear: _selectedYear,
        modeOfAcquisition: _modeOfAcquisition,
        quantity: _editQuantity,
        unitPrice: _editUnitPrice,
        rfidTag: _rfidController.text.trim(),
        conditionStatus: _conditionStatus,
        operationalStatus: _operationalStatus,
      );

      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _assetList[_currentIndex] = updated;
        _newWatermarkedPhotoBytes = null;
        _newPhotoFileName = null;
        _isEditing = false;
        _isUpdating = false;
      });

      widget.onAssetUpdated?.call();
      _loadAuditHistory();

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(
            _operationalStatus == 'Disposed'
                ? 'Item marked as Disposed and moved to Archive.'
                : 'Item record updated successfully.',
          ),
          backgroundColor: ParishColors.oliveGreen,
        ),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isUpdating = false);
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text('Update failed: $e'),
          backgroundColor: ParishColors.mercyRed,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _confirmArchiveAsset() async {
    final reasonController = TextEditingController();
    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        title: Text(
          'Archive Parish Item?',
          style: TextStyle(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            fontSize: 16,
            color: ParishColors.textDark,
          ),
        ),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              'Are you sure you want to archive "${_currentAsset.itemName}" (${_currentAsset.controlNumber})?',
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: ParishColors.textDark),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            TextField(
              controller: reasonController,
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: ParishColors.textDark),
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Reason for Archiving *',
                labelStyle: TextStyle(color: ParishColors.textDark),
                border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                  borderSide: BorderSide(color: ParishColors.borderGrey),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false),
            child: Text(
              'Cancel',
              style: TextStyle(color: ParishColors.textMuted),
            ),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: ParishColors.mercyRed,
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
            ),
            onPressed: () {
              if (reasonController.text.trim().isNotEmpty) {
                Navigator.pop(ctx, true);
              }
            },
            child: const Text('Archive'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    if (confirmed == true && reasonController.text.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      await AssetService.archiveAsset(
        assetId: _currentAsset.assetId,
        reason: reasonController.text.trim(),
      );
      if (!mounted) return;
      widget.onAssetUpdated?.call();
      Navigator.pop(context);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _restoreAsset() async {
    await AssetService.restoreAsset(_currentAsset.assetId);
    if (!mounted) return;
    widget.onAssetUpdated?.call();
    Navigator.pop(context);
  }

  Future<void> _printLabelSticker() async {
    await AssetLabelPdfService.printSingleAssetLabel(_currentAsset);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final asset = _currentAsset;
    final isArchived = asset.isEffectivelyArchived;
    final totalCount = _assetList.length;
    final currentPos = _currentIndex + 1;

    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) {
        final screenWidth = constraints.maxWidth;
        final bool isMobile = screenWidth < 700;

        return Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: ParishColors.backgroundLight,
          appBar: AppBar(
            backgroundColor: ParishColors.cardWhite,
            elevation: 0,
            leading: IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: ParishColors.marianBlue),
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            ),
            title: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Flexible(
                      child: Text(
                        asset.controlNumber,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: isMobile ? 15 : 18,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: isArchived
                              ? ParishColors.mercyRed
                              : ParishColors.marianBlue,
                        ),
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Container(
                      padding:
                      const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: asset.conditionSurfaceColor,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                        border: Border.all(
                          color: asset.conditionColor.withOpacity(0.4),
                        ),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        asset.conditionStatus.toUpperCase(),
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 9,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: asset.conditionColor,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                Text(
                  asset.itemName,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: isMobile ? 11 : 12.5,
                    color: ParishColors.textMuted,
                  ),
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ],
            ),
            actions: [
              Container(
                margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8, horizontal: 8),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: ParishColors.marianBlueSurface,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  border: Border.all(
                    color: ParishColors.marianBlue.withOpacity(0.25),
                  ),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    IconButton(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.chevron_left, size: 22),
                      padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                      constraints:
                      const BoxConstraints(minWidth: 32, minHeight: 32),
                      color: _hasPrevious
                          ? ParishColors.marianBlue
                          : ParishColors.borderGrey,
                      tooltip: 'Previous Item',
                      onPressed: _hasPrevious ? _navigateToPrevious : null,
                    ),
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6),
                      child: Text(
                        '$currentPos / $totalCount',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    IconButton(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 22),
                      padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                      constraints:
                      const BoxConstraints(minWidth: 32, minHeight: 32),
                      color: _hasNext
                          ? ParishColors.marianBlue
                          : ParishColors.borderGrey,
                      tooltip: 'Next Item',
                      onPressed: _hasNext ? _navigateToNext : null,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
            ],
          ),
          body: SafeArea(
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                horizontal: isMobile ? 14 : 24,
                vertical: isMobile ? 14 : 22,
              ),
              child: Center(
                child: ConstrainedBox(
                  constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 960),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      if (isMobile) ...[
                        Row(
                          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                          children: [
                            OutlinedButton.icon(
                              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                    horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
                                side: BorderSide(color: ParishColors.borderGrey),
                              ),
                              onPressed:
                              _hasPrevious ? _navigateToPrevious : null,
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, size: 14),
                              label: const Text('Previous',
                                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11)),
                            ),
                            Text(
                              'Item $currentPos of $totalCount',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                fontSize: 11.5,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                color: ParishColors.textMuted,
                              ),
                            ),
                            OutlinedButton.icon(
                              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                    horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
                                side: BorderSide(color: ParishColors.borderGrey),
                              ),
                              onPressed: _hasNext ? _navigateToNext : null,
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_forward, size: 14),
                              label: const Text('Next',
                                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11)),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      ],

                      if (_isEditing)
                        _buildEditFormCard(isMobile)
                      else
                        _buildViewDetails(isMobile),

                      const SizedBox(height: 18),

                      _buildBottomActionBar(isMobile, isArchived),
                      const SizedBox(height: 36),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // View Details Mode: Executive Card UI for Diocesan Inventory Records

  Widget _buildViewDetails(bool isMobile) {
    final asset = _currentAsset;
    final textDark = ParishColors.textDark;
    final isArchived = asset.isEffectivelyArchived;

    final inventoryDateStr = asset.lastAuditedAt != null
        ? asset.formattedLastAuditedDate
        : asset.formattedRegistrationDate;

    final String costFormatted = asset.quantity > 1
        ? '${asset.formattedTotalCost} (${asset.formattedUnitPrice}/unit)'
        : asset.formattedTotalCost;

    final bool hasOtherDescriptions =
        asset.others != null && asset.others!.trim().isNotEmpty;
    final bool hasRfid =
        asset.rfidTag != null && asset.rfidTag!.trim().isNotEmpty;

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        if (isArchived) ...[
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: ParishColors.mercyRedSurface,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              border: Border.all(color: ParishColors.mercyRed.withOpacity(0.4)),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                const Icon(
                  Icons.archive_outlined,
                  color: ParishColors.mercyRed,
                  size: 22,
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 10),

                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      const Text(
                        'ARCHIVED / DISPOSED ITEM',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: ParishColors.mercyRed,
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        asset.archiveReason != null &&
                            asset.archiveReason!.isNotEmpty
                            ? 'Reason: ${asset.archiveReason}'
                            : 'This item has been officially disposed.',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11.5,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,

                      ),

              ],
            ),

        // 1. HERO HEADER: Watermarked Photo + Canonical Identity & Badges
        Container(
          width: double.infinity,

            color: ParishColors.cardWhite,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
            border: Border.all(color: ParishColors.borderGrey),
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(
                color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.02),
                blurRadius: 8,
                offset: const Offset(0, 2),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          padding: EdgeInsets.all(isMobile ? 14 : 18),
          child: Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              _buildPhotoThumbnail(size: isMobile ? 96 : 130),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,

                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 10, vertical: 4.5),

        Container(
