        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // Bottom Action Bar
  // ===========================================================================

  Widget _buildBottomActionBar(bool isMobile, bool isArchived) {
    return Container(
      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: isMobile ? 12 : 18,
        vertical: 12,
      ),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: ParishColors.cardWhite,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: ParishColors.borderGrey),
      ),
      child: isMobile
          ? Column(
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                    foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10),
                  ),
                  onPressed: _printLabelSticker,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.qr_code, size: 16),
                  label: const Text('Print QR Tag',
                      style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                ),
              ),
              if (_canArchive) ...[
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Expanded(
                  child: isArchived
                      ? OutlinedButton.icon(
                    style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                      foregroundColor: ParishColors.oliveGreen,
                      side: const BorderSide(
                          color: ParishColors.oliveGreen),
                      padding:
                      const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10),
                    ),
                    onPressed: _restoreAsset,
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.restore, size: 16),
                    label: const Text('Restore',
                        style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  )
                      : OutlinedButton.icon(
                    style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                      foregroundColor: ParishColors.mercyRed,
                      side: const BorderSide(
                          color: ParishColors.mercyRed),
                      padding:
                      const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10),
                    ),
                    onPressed: _confirmArchiveAsset,
                    icon:
                    const Icon(Icons.archive_outlined, size: 16),
                    label: const Text('Archive',
                        style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
            children: [
              if (_canModify) ...[
                if (_isEditing) ...[
                  TextButton(
                    onPressed: _isUpdating
                        ? null
                        : () => setState(() => _isEditing = false),
                    child: const Text('Cancel'),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  ElevatedButton.icon(
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: ParishColors.oliveGreen,
                      foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                    ),
                    onPressed: _isUpdating ? null : _saveAssetUpdates,
                    icon: _isUpdating
                        ? const SizedBox(
                      width: 14,
                      height: 14,
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                          color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2),
                    )
                        : const Icon(Icons.save, size: 16),
                    label: Text(_isUpdating ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Changes'),
                  ),
                ] else ...[
                  OutlinedButton.icon(
                    onPressed: () => setState(() => _isEditing = true),
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_outlined, size: 16),
                    label: const Text('Edit Details'),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ],
      )
          : Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              ElevatedButton.icon(
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                  foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                ),
                onPressed: _printLabelSticker,
                icon: const Icon(Icons.qr_code, size: 16),
                label: const Text('Print QR Tag',
                    style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              if (_canArchive)
                isArchived
                    ? OutlinedButton.icon(
                  style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                    foregroundColor: ParishColors.oliveGreen,
                    side: const BorderSide(
                        color: ParishColors.oliveGreen),
                  ),
                  onPressed: _restoreAsset,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.restore, size: 16),
                  label: const Text('Restore Asset'),
                )
                    : OutlinedButton.icon(
                  style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                    foregroundColor: ParishColors.mercyRed,
                    side: const BorderSide(
                        color: ParishColors.mercyRed),
                  ),
                  onPressed: _confirmArchiveAsset,
                  icon:
                  const Icon(Icons.archive_outlined, size: 16),
                  label: const Text('Archive Item'),
                ),
            ],
          ),
          Row(
            children: [
              if (_canModify) ...[
                if (_isEditing) ...[
                  TextButton(
                    onPressed: _isUpdating
                        ? null
                        : () => setState(() => _isEditing = false),
                    child: const Text('Cancel'),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  ElevatedButton.icon(
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: ParishColors.oliveGreen,
                      foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                    ),
                    onPressed: _isUpdating ? null : _saveAssetUpdates,
                    icon: _isUpdating
                        ? const SizedBox(
                      width: 14,
                      height: 14,
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                          color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2),
                    )
                        : const Icon(Icons.save, size: 16),
                    label: Text(_isUpdating ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Changes'),
                  ),
                ] else ...[
                  OutlinedButton.icon(
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      side: const BorderSide(
                          color: ParishColors.marianBlue, width: 1.4),
                      backgroundColor: ParishColors.marianBlueSurface,
                    ),
                    onPressed: () => setState(() => _isEditing = true),
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_outlined,
                        size: 16, color: ParishColors.marianBlue),
                    label: const Text('Edit Details',
                        style: TextStyle(
                            color: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // Helper Widgets
  // ===========================================================================

  Widget _buildPhotoThumbnail({required double size}) {
    final asset = _currentAsset;
    final borderGrey = ParishColors.borderGrey;

    return Tooltip(
      message: asset.photoUrl != null && asset.photoUrl!.isNotEmpty
          ? 'Tap to inspect photo with security watermark'
          : 'No photo uploaded',
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: asset.photoUrl != null && asset.photoUrl!.isNotEmpty
            ? () => _openInteractivePreview(NetworkImage(asset.photoUrl!))
            : null,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        child: Container(
          width: size,
          height: size,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: ParishColors.backgroundLight,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            border: Border.all(
              color:
              asset.photoUrl != null ? ParishColors.goldAccent : borderGrey,
              width: asset.photoUrl != null ? 1.5 : 1.0,
            ),
          ),
          child: ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
            child: asset.photoUrl != null && asset.photoUrl!.isNotEmpty
                ? Stack(
              fit: StackFit.expand,
              children: [
                Image.network(
                  asset.photoUrl!,
                  fit: BoxFit.cover,
                  errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => Center(
                    child: Icon(asset.classificationIcon,
                        size: 36, color: ParishColors.marianBlue),
                  ),
                ),
                Positioned(
                  bottom: 0,
                  left: 0,
                  right: 0,
                  child: Container(
                    color: Colors.black54,
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 2),
                    child: const Text(
                      'WATERMARKED',
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        fontSize: 7.5,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            )
                : Center(
              child: Icon(asset.classificationIcon,
                  size: 36, color: ParishColors.marianBlue),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAuditLogTile(AssetAuditLogModel log) {
    final textDark = ParishColors.textDark;
    final textMuted = ParishColors.textMuted;

    final int totalDistQty = log.quantityDistribution != null
        ? log.quantityDistribution!.fold<int>(0, (sum, d) => sum + d.quantity)
        : 0;
    final bool showDistributionCard = log.hasQuantityDistribution && totalDistQty > 1;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: log.hasQuantityAdjustment
            ? ParishColors.goldLight.withOpacity(0.4)
            : (showDistributionCard
            ? ParishColors.marianBlueSurface.withOpacity(0.3)
            : ParishColors.backgroundLight),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(
          color: log.hasQuantityAdjustment
              ? ParishColors.goldAccent.withOpacity(0.6)
              : (showDistributionCard
              ? ParishColors.marianBlue.withOpacity(0.35)
              : ParishColors.borderGrey),
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Icon(
                log.auditMethod == 'RFID_NFC'
                    ? Icons.nfc
                    : Icons.qr_code_scanner,
                size: 16,
                color: ParishColors.marianBlue,
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          'Condition: ${log.newCondition}',
                          style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 12,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              color: textDark),
                        ),
                        Text(
                          log.formattedAuditedAt,
                          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10.5, color: textMuted),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    if (log.newStatus != null && log.newStatus!.isNotEmpty) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text('Availability: ${log.newStatus}',
                          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: textMuted)),
                    ],
                    if (log.newLocation != null &&
                        log.newLocation!.isNotEmpty) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text('Location: ${log.newLocation}',
                          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: textMuted)),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),

          if (showDistributionCard) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Container(
              width: double.infinity,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                border: Border.all(
                  color: ParishColors.marianBlue.withOpacity(0.3),
                ),
              ),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      const Icon(
                        Icons.pie_chart_outline_rounded,
                        size: 14,
                        color: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 6),
                      Text(
                        'VERIFIED QUANTITY DISTRIBUTION',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 9.5,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          letterSpacing: 0.5,
                          color: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const Spacer(),
                      Text(
                        '$totalDistQty units reconciled',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 10.5,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: ParishColors.textDark,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const Divider(height: 12),
                  Wrap(
                    spacing: 6,
                    runSpacing: 6,
                    children: log.quantityDistribution!.map((dist) {
                      final bool isDamaged =
                          dist.physicalCondition.toLowerCase() == 'damaged';
                      final bool isInUse =
                      dist.utilizationStatus.toLowerCase().contains('use');

                      Color badgeBg;
                      Color badgeBorder;
                      Color badgeText;

                      if (isDamaged) {
                        badgeBg = ParishColors.mercyRedSurface;
                        badgeBorder = ParishColors.mercyRed.withOpacity(0.5);
                        badgeText = ParishColors.mercyRed;
                      } else if (isInUse) {
                        badgeBg = ParishColors.marianBlueSurface;
                        badgeBorder = ParishColors.marianBlue.withOpacity(0.3);
                        badgeText = ParishColors.marianBlue;
                      } else {
                        badgeBg = ParishColors.oliveGreenSurface;
                        badgeBorder = ParishColors.oliveGreen.withOpacity(0.3);
                        badgeText = ParishColors.oliveGreen;
                      }

                      return Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                            horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: badgeBg,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                          border: Border.all(color: badgeBorder),
                        ),
                        child: Row(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            Text(
                              '${dist.quantity}x ',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                fontSize: 11,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                                color: badgeText,
                              ),
                            ),
                            Text(
                              '${dist.utilizationStatus} ',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                fontSize: 11,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                color: ParishColors.textDark,
                              ),
                            ),
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                  horizontal: 4, vertical: 1),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: Colors.white,
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(3),
                                border: Border.all(
                                    color: badgeBorder.withOpacity(0.6)),
                              ),
                              child: Text(
                                dist.physicalCondition,
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  fontSize: 9.5,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  color: badgeText,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      );
                    }).toList(),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],

          if (log.hasQuantityAdjustment) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                border:
                Border.all(color: ParishColors.goldAccent.withOpacity(0.4)),
              ),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Icon(
                        (log.quantityChange ?? 0) > 0
                            ? Icons.add_circle
                            : Icons.remove_circle,
                        size: 14,
                        color: (log.quantityChange ?? 0) > 0
                            ? ParishColors.oliveGreen
                            : ParishColors.mercyRed,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 6),
                      Text(
                        'Official Quantity Adjustment: ${log.formattedQuantityChange}',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11.5,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: (log.quantityChange ?? 0) > 0
                              ? ParishColors.oliveGreen
                              : ParishColors.mercyRed,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  if (log.quantityChangeReason != null &&
                      log.quantityChangeReason!.isNotEmpty) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: 3),
                    Text(
                      'Reason: ${log.quantityChangeReason}',
                      style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: textDark),
                    ),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],

          if (log.cleanNotes.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            Text('Notes: ${log.cleanNotes}',
                style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 11.5,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: textDark)),
          ],
          if (log.auditorName != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Text(
              'Audited by: ${log.auditorName}',
              style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 10,
                  fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: textMuted),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildConditionDropdown() {
    final effective = AssetModel.normalizeCondition(_conditionStatus);
    final items = List<String>.from(_conditionOptions);
    if (!items.contains(effective)) items.add(effective);

    return DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
      value: effective,
      decoration: _floatingInputDecoration(
        labelText: 'Item Condition *',
      ),
      items: items.map((c) {
        final desc = AssetModel.conditionDescriptions[c] ?? '';
        return DropdownMenuItem(
          value: c,
          child: Text(
            desc.isNotEmpty ? '$c ($desc)' : c,
            style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 13,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                color: ParishColors.textDark),
            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
          ),
        );
      }).toList(),
      onChanged: (val) {
        if (val != null) setState(() => _conditionStatus = val);
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildOperationalStatusDropdown() {
    final effective = AssetModel.normalizeStatus(_operationalStatus);
    final items = List<String>.from(_operationalStatusOptions);
    if (!items.contains(effective)) items.add(effective);

    return DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
      value: effective,
      decoration: _floatingInputDecoration(
        labelText: 'Availability *',
      ),
      items: items.map((s) {
        final desc = AssetModel.availabilityDescriptions[s] ?? '';
        return DropdownMenuItem(
          value: s,
          child: Text(
            desc.isNotEmpty ? '$s — $desc' : s,
            style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 13,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                color: ParishColors.textDark),
            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
          ),
        );
      }).toList(),
      onChanged: (val) {
        if (val != null) setState(() => _operationalStatus = val);
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAcquisitionModeDropdown() {
    return DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
      value: _modeOfAcquisition,
      isExpanded: true,
      decoration: _floatingInputDecoration(
        labelText: 'How Acquired *',
      ),
      items: _acquisitionModes
          .map((m) => DropdownMenuItem(value: m, child: Text(m)))
          .toList(),
      onChanged: (val) => setState(() => _modeOfAcquisition = val!),
    );
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// FILE NAME: asset_drafts_page.dart
// FILE PATH: lib/features/asset_inventory/presentation/pages/asset_drafts_page.dart
// =============================================================================

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/asset_inventory/presentation/pages/asset_drafts_page.dart
// =============================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../../core/constants/colors.dart';
import '../../models/asset_draft_model.dart';
import '../../services/asset_draft_service.dart';
import 'register_asset_page.dart';

class AssetDraftsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final VoidCallback? onAssetSaved;

  const AssetDraftsPage({super.key, this.onAssetSaved});

  @override
  State<AssetDraftsPage> createState() => _AssetDraftsPageState();
}

class _AssetDraftsPageState extends State<AssetDraftsPage> {
  List<AssetDraftModel> _drafts = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadDrafts();
  }

  Future<void> _loadDrafts() async {
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    final drafts = await AssetDraftService.getAllDrafts();
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _drafts = drafts;
      _isLoading = false;
    });
  }

  Future<void> _deleteDraft(AssetDraftModel draft) async {
    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        title: Text(
          'Delete Registration Draft?',
          style: TextStyle(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: ParishColors.textDark,
            fontSize: 16,
          ),
        ),
        content: Text(
          'Are you sure you want to discard the cloud draft for "${draft.itemName.isNotEmpty ? draft.itemName : draft.fullControlNumber}"? '
              'This will remove it from all devices.',
          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: ParishColors.textDark),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false),
            child: Text('Cancel', style: TextStyle(color: ParishColors.textMuted)),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: ParishColors.mercyRed,
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
            ),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
            child: const Text('Delete Draft'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    if (confirmed == true) {
      await AssetDraftService.deleteDraft(draft.draftId);
      await _loadDrafts();
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
         SnackBar(
          content: Text('Cloud draft deleted.'),
          backgroundColor: ParishColors.textMuted,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _clearAllDrafts() async {
    if (_drafts.isEmpty) return;

    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        title: Text(
          'Clear All Saved Drafts?',
          style: TextStyle(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: ParishColors.textDark,
            fontSize: 16,
          ),
        ),
        content: Text(
          'Are you sure you want to discard all ${_drafts.length} cloud registration drafts? This cannot be undone.',
          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: ParishColors.textDark),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false),
            child: Text('Cancel', style: TextStyle(color: ParishColors.textMuted)),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: ParishColors.mercyRed,
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
            ),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
            child: const Text('Clear All'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    if (confirmed == true) {
      await AssetDraftService.clearAllDrafts();
      await _loadDrafts();
    }
  }

  void _resumeRegistration(AssetDraftModel draft) {
    Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (_) => RegisterAssetPage(
          initialDraft: draft,
          onAssetSaved: () {
            _loadDrafts();
            widget.onAssetSaved?.call();
          },
        ),
      ),
    ).then((_) => _loadDrafts());
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) {
        final bool isMobile = constraints.maxWidth < 650;

        return Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: ParishColors.backgroundLight,
          appBar: AppBar(
            backgroundColor: ParishColors.cardWhite,
            elevation: 0,
            leading: IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: ParishColors.marianBlue),
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            ),
            title: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Cloud Asset Drafts',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: isMobile ? 16 : 18,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: ParishColors.textDark,
                  ),
                ),
                Text(
                  'Synchronized across all parish phones, tablets, and laptops',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: isMobile ? 10.5 : 12,
                    color: ParishColors.textMuted,
                  ),
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ],
            ),
            actions: [
              if (_drafts.isNotEmpty)
                TextButton.icon(
                  onPressed: _clearAllDrafts,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_sweep_outlined,
                      size: 16, color: ParishColors.mercyRed),
                  label: Text(
                    'Clear All',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: isMobile ? 11.5 : 12.5,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: ParishColors.mercyRed,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
            ],
          ),
          body: _isLoading
              ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
              : _drafts.isEmpty
              ? _buildEmptyState(isMobile)
              : RefreshIndicator(
            onRefresh: _loadDrafts,
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
              padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                horizontal: isMobile ? 14 : 24,
                vertical: isMobile ? 14 : 20,
              ),
              child: Center(
                child: ConstrainedBox(
                  constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 860),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      _buildDraftsHeader(isMobile),
                      const SizedBox(height: 14),
                      ..._drafts
                          .map((d) => _buildDraftCard(d, isMobile)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDraftsHeader(bool isMobile) {
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: ParishColors.cardWhite,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: ParishColors.borderGrey),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(Icons.cloud_outlined,
                  size: 18, color: ParishColors.marianBlue),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text(
                '${_drafts.length} Cloud Registration Draft(s)',
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: ParishColors.textDark,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          Text(
            'Synced via Supabase',
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: ParishColors.textMuted),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDraftCard(AssetDraftModel draft, bool isMobile) {
    final bool hasCloudPhoto =
        draft.photoUrl != null && draft.photoUrl!.isNotEmpty;
    final bool hasLocalPhoto =
        draft.localPhotoBytes != null && draft.localPhotoBytes!.isNotEmpty;
    final bool hasAnyPhoto = hasCloudPhoto || hasLocalPhoto;
    final List<String> missing = draft.missingChecklist;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: ParishColors.cardWhite,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: ParishColors.borderGrey),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.02),
            blurRadius: 6,
            offset: const Offset(0, 2),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(isMobile ? 12 : 16),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              // Photo Thumbnail (Cloud Network Image or Local In-Memory Fallback)
              Container(
                width: isMobile ? 54 : 64,
                height: isMobile ? 54 : 64,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: ParishColors.backgroundLight,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  border: Border.all(
                    color: hasAnyPhoto
                        ? ParishColors.goldAccent
                        : ParishColors.borderGrey,
                    width: hasAnyPhoto ? 1.4 : 1.0,
                  ),
                ),
                child: ClipRRect(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),
                  child: hasCloudPhoto
                      ? Image.network(
                    draft.photoUrl!,
                    fit: BoxFit.cover,
                    errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => Icon(
                      Icons.broken_image_outlined,
                      size: 24,
                      color: ParishColors.textMuted.withOpacity(0.6),
                    ),
                  )
                      : hasLocalPhoto
                      ? Image.memory(draft.localPhotoBytes!,
                      fit: BoxFit.cover)
                      : Icon(
                    Icons.image_not_supported_outlined,
                    size: 24,
                    color: ParishColors.textMuted.withOpacity(0.6),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),

              // Title, Control # and Metadata
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: ParishColors.goldLight,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                            border: Border.all(
                              color: ParishColors.goldAccent.withOpacity(0.6),
                            ),
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            draft.fullControlNumber,
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              fontFamily: 'Courier',
                              fontSize: 10.5,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              color: ParishColors.goldAccent,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 6),
                        Text(
                          '${draft.quantity} unit(s)',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 10.5,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            color: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(
                      draft.itemName.trim().isNotEmpty
                          ? draft.itemName
                          : 'Untitled Draft Entry',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: isMobile ? 14 : 15.5,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: draft.itemName.trim().isNotEmpty
                            ? ParishColors.textDark
                            : ParishColors.textMuted,
                      ),
                      maxLines: 1,
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 2),
                    Text(
                      'Category: ${draft.classificationAcronym} • Location: ${draft.locationAcronym} • Mode: ${draft.modeOfAcquisition}',
                      style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11, color: ParishColors.textMuted),
                      maxLines: 1,
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),

          // Missing Checklist Tags
          Wrap(
            spacing: 6,
            runSpacing: 4,
            crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
            children: [
              if (missing.isEmpty)
                Container(
                  padding:
                  const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 7, vertical: 2),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: ParishColors.oliveGreenSurface,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                    border: Border.all(
                        color: ParishColors.oliveGreen.withOpacity(0.4)),
                  ),
                  child: const Row(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.check_circle,
                          size: 12, color: ParishColors.oliveGreen),
                      SizedBox(width: 4),
                      Text(
                        'Ready to Register',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 10,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: ParishColors.oliveGreen,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                )
              else
                ...missing.map(
                      (m) => Container(
                    padding:
                    const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: const Color(0xFFFFFBEB),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                      border: Border.all(
                          color: const Color(0xFFF59E0B).withOpacity(0.4)),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      '⚠️ Needs $m',
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 9.5,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: Color(0xFFD97706),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
          const Divider(height: 18),

          // Bottom Bar: Last Updated & Action Buttons
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(
                'Updated: ${draft.formattedUpdatedAt}',
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10.5, color: ParishColors.textMuted),
              ),
              Row(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  IconButton(
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline,
                        size: 18, color: ParishColors.mercyRed),
                    tooltip: 'Discard Draft',
                    onPressed: () => _deleteDraft(draft),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 4),
                  ElevatedButton.icon(
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: ParishColors.marianBlue,
                      foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                    ),
                    onPressed: () => _resumeRegistration(draft),
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_note, size: 16),
                    label: const Text(
                      'Resume',
                      style:
                      TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildEmptyState(bool isMobile) {
    return Center(
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.all(28),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: ParishColors.cardWhite,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          border: Border.all(color: ParishColors.borderGrey),
        ),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.cloud_done_outlined,
                size: 48, color: ParishColors.textMuted.withOpacity(0.6)),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(
              'No Saved Cloud Drafts',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 16,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                color: ParishColors.textDark,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            Text(
              'Use the "Save as Cloud Draft" button during item registration to store incomplete work across devices.',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: ParishColors.textMuted),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// FILE NAME: asset_references_page.dart
// FILE PATH: lib/features/asset_inventory/presentation/pages/asset_references_page.dart
// =============================================================================

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/asset_inventory/presentation/pages/asset_references_page.dart
// =============================================================================

import 'dart:math';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../../core/constants/colors.dart';
import '../../models/asset_reference_models.dart';
import '../../services/asset_reference_service.dart';

class AssetReferencesPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final VoidCallback? onReferencesUpdated;

  const AssetReferencesPage({super.key, this.onReferencesUpdated});

  @override
  State<AssetReferencesPage> createState() => _AssetReferencesPageState();
}

class _AssetReferencesPageState extends State<AssetReferencesPage> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late TabController _tabController;
  List<AssetLocationModel> _locations = [];
  List<AssetClassificationModel> _classifications = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;
  String? _errorMessage;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tabController = TabController(length: 2, vsync: this);
    _loadReferences();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tabController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _loadReferences() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _errorMessage = null;
    });

    try {
      final locs = await AssetReferenceService.getLocations(activeOnly: false);
      final classifs = await AssetReferenceService.getClassifications(activeOnly: false);
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _locations = locs;
        _classifications = classifs;
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _errorMessage = e.toString().replaceFirst('Exception: ', '');
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  void _openLocationFormDialog([AssetLocationModel? itemToEdit]) {
    final nameController = TextEditingController(text: itemToEdit?.locationName ?? '');
    final acronymController = TextEditingController(text: itemToEdit?.acronym ?? '');
    final descController = TextEditingController(text: itemToEdit?.description ?? '');
    bool isActive = itemToEdit?.isActive ?? true;
    final isEdit = itemToEdit != null;
    final formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) {
        final screenWidth = MediaQuery.of(ctx).size.width;

        return StatefulBuilder(
          builder: (context, setModalState) => AlertDialog(
            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
            title: Text(
              isEdit ? 'Edit Location' : 'Add New Location',
              style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16, color: ParishColors.textDark),
            ),
            content: ConstrainedBox(
              constraints: BoxConstraints(maxWidth: min(screenWidth - 48, 440)),
              child: Form(
                key: formKey,
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: acronymController,
                      enabled: !isEdit,
                      textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.characters,
                      style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: ParishColors.textDark),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Acronym * (e.g. C, S, PEAC)',
                        labelStyle: TextStyle(color: ParishColors.textDark),
                        hintText: 'e.g. C, S, PEAC',
                        hintStyle: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, color: ParishColors.textMuted),
                        border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: BorderSide(color: ParishColors.borderGrey)),
                      ),
                      validator: (v) => (v?.trim().isEmpty ?? true) ? 'Acronym is required.' : null,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: nameController,
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, color: ParishColors.textDark),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Location Name * (e.g. Church)',
                        labelStyle: TextStyle(color: ParishColors.textDark),
                        hintText: 'e.g. Church Main Nave',
                        hintStyle: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, color: ParishColors.textMuted),
                        border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: BorderSide(color: ParishColors.borderGrey)),
                      ),
                      validator: (v) => (v?.trim().isEmpty ?? true) ? 'Location name is required.' : null,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: descController,
                      maxLines: 2,
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: ParishColors.textDark),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Description (Optional)',
                        labelStyle: TextStyle(color: ParishColors.textDark),
                        hintText: 'e.g. Main church sanctuary and altar area',
                        hintStyle: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, color: ParishColors.textMuted),
                        border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: BorderSide(color: ParishColors.borderGrey)),
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (isEdit) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      SwitchListTile(
                        contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                        activeColor: ParishColors.oliveGreen,
                        title: const Text('Active Status', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                        value: isActive,
                        onChanged: (val) => setModalState(() => isActive = val),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            actions: [
              TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: Text('Cancel', style: TextStyle(color: ParishColors.textMuted))),
              ElevatedButton(
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: ParishColors.marianBlue, foregroundColor: Colors.white),
                onPressed: () async {
                  if (!formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
                  try {
                    if (isEdit) {
                      await AssetReferenceService.updateLocation(
                        locationId: itemToEdit.locationId,
                        locationName: nameController.text.trim(),
                        description: descController.text.trim(),
                        isActive: isActive,
                      );
                    } else {
                      await AssetReferenceService.createLocation(
                        acronym: acronymController.text.trim(),
                        locationName: nameController.text.trim(),
                        description: descController.text.trim(),
                      );
                    }
                    if (!mounted) return;
                    Navigator.pop(ctx);
                    _loadReferences();
                    widget.onReferencesUpdated?.call();
                  } catch (e) {
                    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Error: $e')));
                  }
                },
                child: Text(isEdit ? 'Update' : 'Save Location'),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _openClassificationFormDialog([AssetClassificationModel? itemToEdit]) {
    final nameController = TextEditingController(text: itemToEdit?.classificationName ?? '');
    final acronymController = TextEditingController(text: itemToEdit?.acronym ?? '');
    final descController = TextEditingController(text: itemToEdit?.description ?? '');
    bool isActive = itemToEdit?.isActive ?? true;
    final isEdit = itemToEdit != null;
    final formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) {
        final screenWidth = MediaQuery.of(ctx).size.width;

        return StatefulBuilder(
          builder: (context, setModalState) => AlertDialog(
            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
            title: Text(
              isEdit ? 'Edit Classification' : 'Add New Classification',
              style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16, color: ParishColors.textDark),
            ),
            content: ConstrainedBox(
              constraints: BoxConstraints(maxWidth: min(screenWidth - 48, 440)),
              child: Form(
                key: formKey,
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: acronymController,
                      enabled: !isEdit,
                      textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.characters,
                      style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: ParishColors.textDark),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Acronym * (e.g. SI, SV, FF)',
                        labelStyle: TextStyle(color: ParishColors.textDark),
                        hintText: 'e.g. SI, SV, FF',
                        hintStyle: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, color: ParishColors.textMuted),
                        border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: BorderSide(color: ParishColors.borderGrey)),
                      ),
                      validator: (v) => (v?.trim().isEmpty ?? true) ? 'Acronym is required.' : null,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: nameController,
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, color: ParishColors.textDark),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Classification Name * (e.g. Sacred Image)',
                        labelStyle: TextStyle(color: ParishColors.textDark),
                        hintText: 'e.g. Sacred Image',
                        hintStyle: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, color: ParishColors.textMuted),
                        border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: BorderSide(color: ParishColors.borderGrey)),
                      ),
                      validator: (v) => (v?.trim().isEmpty ?? true) ? 'Classification name is required.' : null,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: descController,
                      maxLines: 2,
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: ParishColors.textDark),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Description (Optional)',
                        labelStyle: TextStyle(color: ParishColors.textDark),
                        hintText: 'e.g. Statues, sacred representations and icons',
                        hintStyle: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, color: ParishColors.textMuted),
                        border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: BorderSide(color: ParishColors.borderGrey)),
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (isEdit) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      SwitchListTile(
                        contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                        activeColor: ParishColors.oliveGreen,
                        title: const Text('Active Status', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                        value: isActive,
                        onChanged: (val) => setModalState(() => isActive = val),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            actions: [
              TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: Text('Cancel', style: TextStyle(color: ParishColors.textMuted))),
              ElevatedButton(
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: ParishColors.marianBlue, foregroundColor: Colors.white),
                onPressed: () async {
                  if (!formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
                  try {
                    if (isEdit) {
                      await AssetReferenceService.updateClassification(
                        classificationId: itemToEdit.classificationId,
                        classificationName: nameController.text.trim(),
                        description: descController.text.trim(),
                        isActive: isActive,
                      );
                    } else {
                      await AssetReferenceService.createClassification(
                        acronym: acronymController.text.trim(),
                        classificationName: nameController.text.trim(),
                        description: descController.text.trim(),
                      );
                    }
                    if (!mounted) return;
                    Navigator.pop(ctx);
                    _loadReferences();
                    widget.onReferencesUpdated?.call();
                  } catch (e) {
                    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Error: $e')));
                  }
                },
                child: Text(isEdit ? 'Update' : 'Save Classification'),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final textDark = ParishColors.textDark;
    final cardWhite = ParishColors.cardWhite;
    final textMuted = ParishColors.textMuted;

    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) {
        final bool isMobile = constraints.maxWidth < 620;

        return Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: ParishColors.backgroundLight,
          appBar: AppBar(
            backgroundColor: cardWhite,
            elevation: 0,
            leading: IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: ParishColors.marianBlue),
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            ),
            title: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Locations & Classifications Management',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: isMobile ? 16 : 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: textDark),
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
                Text(
                  'Master reference data used to auto-generate diocesan control numbers',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: isMobile ? 10.5 : 12, color: textMuted),
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ],
            ),
            bottom: TabBar(
              controller: _tabController,
              labelColor: ParishColors.marianBlue,
              indicatorColor: ParishColors.marianBlue,
              tabs: [
                Tab(text: 'Locations (${_locations.length})'),
                Tab(text: 'Classifications (${_classifications.length})'),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          body: _isLoading
              ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
              : _errorMessage != null
              ? Center(child: Text(_errorMessage!, style: TextStyle(color: ParishColors.mercyRed)))
              : TabBarView(
            controller: _tabController,
            children: [
              _buildLocationsList(isMobile),
              _buildClassificationsList(isMobile),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLocationsList(bool isMobile) {
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(isMobile ? 14 : 20),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  'Parish Storage Locations',
                  style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: isMobile ? 14 : 16, color: ParishColors.textDark),
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              ElevatedButton.icon(
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: ParishColors.oliveGreen,
                  foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                  padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: isMobile ? 10 : 14, vertical: isMobile ? 8 : 10),
                ),
                onPressed: () => _openLocationFormDialog(),
                icon: const Icon(Icons.add, size: 16),
                label: const Text('Add Location'),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          ListView.separated(
            shrinkWrap: true,
            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            itemCount: _locations.length,
            separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(height: 8),
            itemBuilder: (context, idx) {
              final loc = _locations[idx];
              return Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: ParishColors.cardWhite,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  border: Border.all(color: ParishColors.borderGrey),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      width: 44,
                      height: 44,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: ParishColors.marianBlueSurface, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                      child: Center(
                        child: Text(loc.acronym, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: ParishColors.marianBlue)),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 14),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            loc.locationName,
                            style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14, color: ParishColors.textDark),
                            maxLines: 1,
                            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          ),
                          if (loc.description != null && loc.description!.isNotEmpty)
                            Text(
                              loc.description!,
                              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: ParishColors.textMuted),
                              maxLines: 1,
                              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                            ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    IconButton(
                      icon: Icon(Icons.edit_outlined, size: 18, color: ParishColors.marianBlue),
                      onPressed: () => _openLocationFormDialog(loc),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildClassificationsList(bool isMobile) {
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(isMobile ? 14 : 20),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  'Property Classifications',
                  style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: isMobile ? 14 : 16, color: ParishColors.textDark),
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              ElevatedButton.icon(
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: ParishColors.oliveGreen,
                  foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                  padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: isMobile ? 10 : 14, vertical: isMobile ? 8 : 10),
                ),
                onPressed: () => _openClassificationFormDialog(),
                icon: const Icon(Icons.add, size: 16),
                label: const Text('Add Classification'),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          ListView.separated(
            shrinkWrap: true,
            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            itemCount: _classifications.length,
            separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(height: 8),
            itemBuilder: (context, idx) {
              final cls = _classifications[idx];
              return Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: ParishColors.cardWhite,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  border: Border.all(color: ParishColors.borderGrey),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      width: 44,
                      height: 44,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: ParishColors.goldLight, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                      child: Center(
                        child: Text(cls.acronym, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: ParishColors.goldAccent)),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 14),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            cls.classificationName,
                            style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14, color: ParishColors.textDark),
                            maxLines: 1,
                            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          ),
                          if (cls.description != null && cls.description!.isNotEmpty)
                            Text(
                              cls.description!,
                              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: ParishColors.textMuted),
                              maxLines: 1,
                              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                            ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    IconButton(
                      icon: Icon(Icons.edit_outlined, size: 18, color: ParishColors.marianBlue),
                      onPressed: () => _openClassificationFormDialog(cls),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
