// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/asset_inventory/services/asset_report_pdf_service.dart
// =============================================================================

import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:pdf/pdf.dart';
import 'package:pdf/widgets.dart' as pw;
import 'package:printing/printing.dart';
import '../models/asset_model.dart';

class AssetReportPdfService {
  AssetReportPdfService._();

  static const PdfColor marianBlue = PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF164E87);
  static const PdfColor marianNavy = PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF0F2942);
  static const PdfColor goldAccent = PdfColor.fromInt(0xFFD49B18);
  static const PdfColor textDark = PdfColors.black;
  static const PdfColor textMuted = PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF475569);
  static const PdfColor borderGrey = PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF94A3B8);
  static const PdfColor lightCardBg = PdfColor.fromInt(0xFFF8FAFC);

  // Exact card dimensions calibrated so 2 cards fit safely on A4 without clipping
  static const double cardHeight = 352.0;

  // In-memory static caches for high-speed instant re-renders
  static pw.Font? _cachedFontRegular;
  static pw.Font? _cachedFontBold;
  static pw.Font? _cachedFontItalic;
  static pw.Font? _cachedFontBoldItalic;
  static final Map<String, Uint8List> _globalImageCache = {};

  /// Loads and caches all required Google Fonts concurrently
  static Future<List<pw.Font>> _loadFontsConcurrently() async {
    if (_cachedFontRegular != null &&
        _cachedFontBold != null &&
        _cachedFontItalic != null &&
        _cachedFontBoldItalic != null) {
      return [
        _cachedFontRegular!,
        _cachedFontBold!,
        _cachedFontItalic!,
        _cachedFontBoldItalic!,
      ];
    }

    final results = await Future.wait([
      PdfGoogleFonts.arimoRegular(),
      PdfGoogleFonts.arimoBold(),
      PdfGoogleFonts.arimoItalic(),
      PdfGoogleFonts.arimoBoldItalic(),
    ]);

    _cachedFontRegular = results[0];
    _cachedFontBold = results[1];
    _cachedFontItalic = results[2];
    _cachedFontBoldItalic = results[3];

    return results;
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // 1. Photographic Inventory Cards Report (2 Cards per A4 Page)
  // ===========================================================================

  /// Generates the photographic inventory report (2 items per A4 portrait page)
  /// matching the specified reference photo card design.
  static Future<Uint8List> generatePhotographicReportPdf(
      List<AssetModel> assets, {
        String? dateRangeLabel,
        String? sectionLabel,
      }) async {
    const pageFormat = PdfPageFormat.a4;

    // Load fonts and preload all asset photographs in parallel
    final fontsFuture = _loadFontsConcurrently();

    final Map<String, Uint8List?> photoCache = {};
    final photoFetchFutures = assets
        .where((a) => a.photoUrl != null && a.photoUrl!.trim().isNotEmpty)
        .map((asset) async {
      final url = asset.photoUrl!.trim();
      if (_globalImageCache.containsKey(url)) {
        photoCache[asset.assetId] = _globalImageCache[url];
        return;
      }
      try {
        final res = await http.get(Uri.parse(url)).timeout(
          const Duration(seconds: 4),
        );
        if (res.statusCode == 200 && res.bodyBytes.isNotEmpty) {
          _globalImageCache[url] = res.bodyBytes;
          photoCache[asset.assetId] = res.bodyBytes;
        }
      } catch (_) {
        photoCache[asset.assetId] = null;
      }
    });

    final results = await Future.wait([
      fontsFuture,
      Future.wait(photoFetchFutures),
    ]);

    final fonts = results[0] as List<pw.Font>;
    final fontRegular = fonts[0];
    final fontBold = fonts[1];
    final fontItalic = fonts[2];
    final fontBoldItalic = fonts[3];

    final pdf = pw.Document(
      theme: pw.ThemeData.withFont(
        base: fontRegular,
        bold: fontBold,
        italic: fontItalic,
        boldItalic: fontBoldItalic,
      ),
    );

    const int itemsPerPage = 2;
    for (int i = 0; i < assets.length; i += itemsPerPage) {
      final pageAssets = assets.sublist(
        i,
        (i + itemsPerPage > assets.length) ? assets.length : i + itemsPerPage,
      );

      pdf.addPage(
        pw.Page(
          pageFormat: pageFormat,
          margin: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(
            horizontal: 8.0 * PdfPageFormat.mm,
            vertical: 6.0 * PdfPageFormat.mm,
          ),
          build: (context) {
            return pw.Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                // Compact Header
                _buildReportHeader(
                  title: 'PARISH BOOK OF INVENTORY — PHOTOGRAPHIC INVENTORY',
                  dateRangeLabel: dateRangeLabel,
                  sectionLabel: sectionLabel,
                  fontRegular: fontRegular,
                  fontBold: fontBold,
                ),
                pw.SizedBox(height: 5),

                // Cards List (2 items per page)
                ...pageAssets.map((asset) {
                  final imageBytes = photoCache[asset.assetId];
                  final isLastOnPage =
                      pageAssets.indexOf(asset) == pageAssets.length - 1;

                  return pw.Padding(
                    padding: pw.EdgeInsets.only(bottom: isLastOnPage ? 0 : 16),
                    child: pw.SizedBox(
                      height: cardHeight,
                      child: _buildPhotographicCard(
                        asset,
                        imageBytes,
                        fontRegular,
                        fontBold,
                        fontItalic,
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                }),

                pw.Spacer(),

                // Page numbering footer
                pw.Align(
                  alignment: pw.Alignment.centerRight,
                  child: pw.Text(
                    'Page ${context.pageNumber} of ${context.pagesCount}',
                    style: pw.TextStyle(
                      font: fontRegular,
                      fontSize: 7.5,
                      color: textMuted,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      );
    }

    return pdf.save();
  }

  /// Builds a single photographic inventory card matching the exact boxed form layout:
  /// Left: Photo. Right: Boxed form with black ink and full underlined rows.
  static pw.Widget _buildPhotographicCard(
      AssetModel asset,
      Uint8List? imageBytes,
      pw.Font fontRegular,
      pw.Font fontBold,
      pw.Font fontItalic,
      ) {
    final inventoryDateStr = asset.lastAuditedAt != null
        ? asset.formattedLastAuditedDate
        : asset.formattedRegistrationDate;

    // Cost of Acquisition formatting rule:
    // If quantity == 1: "P96,000"
    // If quantity > 1:  "P96,000 (P1,000 per unit)"
    final String formattedTotalOnly =
        'P${AssetModel.formatCurrency(asset.totalCost)}';
    final String formattedCostOfAcquisition = asset.quantity > 1
        ? '$formattedTotalOnly (P${AssetModel.formatCurrency(asset.unitPrice)} per unit)'
        : formattedTotalOnly;

    // Remarks rule: If quantified > 1, put quantity on remarks line
    final String remarksLead =
    asset.quantity > 1 ? 'Quantity: ${asset.quantity} units. ' : '';
    final String baseRemarks =
    asset.remarks != null && asset.remarks!.isNotEmpty
        ? asset.remarks!
        : 'Condition: ${asset.conditionStatus.toUpperCase()} • Availability: ${asset.operationalStatus}';
    final String fullRemarksText = '$remarksLead$baseRemarks';

    return pw.Container(
      decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
        color: PdfColors.white,
        border: pw.Border.all(color: PdfColors.black, width: 0.8),
      ),
      child: pw.Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          // Left side: Item Photograph
          pw.Expanded(
            flex: 10,
            child: pw.Container(
              padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(6),
              color: PdfColors.grey100,
              child: imageBytes != null
                  ? pw.Image(
                pw.MemoryImage(imageBytes),
                fit: pw.BoxFit.contain,
              )
                  : pw.Center(
                child: pw.Column(
                  mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    pw.Text(
                      '[ NO PHOTO ATTACHED ]',
                      style: pw.TextStyle(
                        font: fontBold,
                        fontSize: 10,
                        color: textMuted,
                      ),
                    ),
                    pw.SizedBox(height: 4),
                    pw.Text(
                      asset.itemName,
                      style: pw.TextStyle(
                        font: fontRegular,
                        fontSize: 8.5,
                        color: textMuted,
                      ),
                      textAlign: pw.TextAlign.center,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),

          // Right side: Boxed Evidence Form matching reference image
          pw.Expanded(
            flex: 12,
            child: pw.Container(
              margin: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(4),
              decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
                border: pw.Border.all(color: PdfColors.black, width: 0.8),
              ),
              padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(8, 6, 8, 4),
              child: pw.Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  // Top Header: Control No. and Date of Inventory
                  pw.Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                    children: [
                      pw.Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.end,
                        children: [
                          pw.Text(
                            'Control No.: ',
                            style: pw.TextStyle(
                              font: fontBold,
                              fontSize: 9.0,
                              color: PdfColors.black,
                            ),
                          ),
                          pw.Container(
                            width: 135,
                            decoration: const pw.BoxDecoration(
                              border: pw.Border(
                                bottom: pw.BorderSide(
                                    color: PdfColors.black, width: 0.8),
                              ),
                            ),
                            padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 0.5),
                            child: pw.Text(
                              asset.controlNumber,
                              style: pw.TextStyle(
                                font: fontBold,
                                fontSize: 9.0,
                                color: PdfColors.black,
                              ),
                              textAlign: pw.TextAlign.center,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      pw.SizedBox(height: 1.5),
                      pw.Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.end,
                        children: [
                          pw.Text(
                            'Date of Inventory: ',
                            style: pw.TextStyle(
                              font: fontItalic,
                              fontSize: 9.0,
                              color: PdfColors.black,
                            ),
                          ),
                          pw.Container(
                            width: 135,
                            decoration: const pw.BoxDecoration(
                              border: pw.Border(
                                bottom: pw.BorderSide(
                                    color: PdfColors.black, width: 0.8),
                              ),
                            ),
                            padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 0.5),
                            child: pw.Text(
                              inventoryDateStr,
                              style: pw.TextStyle(
                                font: fontBold,
                                fontSize: 8.5,
                                color: PdfColors.black,
                              ),
                              textAlign: pw.TextAlign.center,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),

                  // Main Properties
                  _buildFormLine(
                    'Item:',
                    asset.itemName,
                    fontBold,
                    fontBold,
                  ),
                  _buildFormLine(
                    'Location:',
                    asset.displayLocation,
                    fontBold,
                    fontBold,
                  ),
                  _buildFormLine(
                    'Date Acquired:',
                    asset.formattedAcquisitionDate,
                    fontBold,
                    fontBold,
                  ),
                  _buildFormLine(
                    'Mode of Acquisition:',
                    asset.modeOfAcquisition,
                    fontBold,
                    fontBold,
                  ),
                  _buildFormLine(
                    'Cost of Acquisition:',
                    formattedCostOfAcquisition,
                    fontBold,
                    fontBold,
                  ),

                  // Description Block
                  pw.Text(
                    'Description:',
                    style: pw.TextStyle(
                      font: fontBold,
                      fontSize: 8.5,
                      color: PdfColors.black,
                    ),
                  ),
                  pw.Padding(
                    padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(left: 16),
                    child: pw.Column(
                      children: [
                        _buildFormLine(
                          'Dimension:',
                          asset.dimensions ?? '',
                          fontItalic,
                          fontBold,
                        ),
                        _buildFormLine(
                          'Color:',
                          asset.color ?? '',
                          fontItalic,
                          fontBold,
                        ),
                        _buildFormLine(
                          'Model:',
                          asset.model ?? '',
                          fontItalic,
                          fontBold,
                        ),
                        _buildFormLine(
                          'Others:',
                          asset.others ?? '',
                          fontItalic,
                          fontBold,
                        ),
                        // 2 blank underline lines under Others
                        _buildEmptyUnderline(),
                        _buildEmptyUnderline(),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),

                  // Remarks Section
                  pw.Text(
                    'Remarks:',
                    style: pw.TextStyle(
                      font: fontBold,
                      fontSize: 8.5,
                      color: PdfColors.black,
                    ),
                  ),
                  pw.Padding(
                    padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(left: 16),
                    child: pw.Column(
                      children: [
                        _buildUnderlinedTextLine(
                          fullRemarksText,
                          fontBold,
                        ),
                        _buildEmptyUnderline(),
                        _buildEmptyUnderline(),
                        _buildEmptyUnderline(),
                        _buildEmptyUnderline(),
                        pw.SizedBox(height: 30),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      );
  }


  /// Builds a form label followed by an underline with bold black answer
  static pw.Widget _buildFormLine(
      String label,
      String value,
      pw.Font labelFont,
      pw.Font valueFont,
      ) {
    return pw.Padding(
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 0.5),
      child: pw.Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.end,
        children: [
          pw.Text(
            label,
            style: pw.TextStyle(
              font: labelFont,
              fontSize: 8.5,
              color: PdfColors.black,
            ),
          ),
          pw.SizedBox(width: 4),
          pw.Expanded(
            child: pw.Container(
              decoration: const pw.BoxDecoration(
                border: pw.Border(
                  bottom: pw.BorderSide(color: PdfColors.black, width: 0.65),
                ),
              ),
              padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 0.5),
              child: pw.Text(
                value,
                style: pw.TextStyle(
                  font: valueFont,
                  fontSize: 8.2,
                  color: PdfColors.black,
                ),
                maxLines: 1,
                overflow: pw.TextOverflow.clip,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Builds an underlined line containing text for remarks
  static pw.Widget _buildUnderlinedTextLine(String text, pw.Font font) {
    return pw.Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      decoration: const pw.BoxDecoration(
        border: pw.Border(
          bottom: pw.BorderSide(color: PdfColors.black, width: 0.65),
        ),
      ),
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 0.5),
      child: pw.Text(
        text,
        style: pw.TextStyle(
          font: font,
          fontSize: 7.8,
          color: PdfColors.black,
        ),
        maxLines: 1,
        overflow: pw.TextOverflow.clip,
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Builds a blank underline matching the notebook-style underlines in the image
  static pw.Widget _buildEmptyUnderline() {
    return pw.Container(
      height: 7.8,
      width: double.infinity,
      decoration: const pw.BoxDecoration(
        border: pw.Border(
          bottom: pw.BorderSide(color: PdfColors.black, width: 0.65),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // 2. Comprehensive 13-Column Tabular Ledger Report (A4 Landscape)
  // ===========================================================================

  /// Generates the canonical 13-column table report on standard A4 Landscape.
  static Future<Uint8List> generateTableReportPdf(
      List<AssetModel> assets, {
        String? dateRangeLabel,
        String? sectionLabel,
      }) async {
    final pageFormat = PdfPageFormat.a4.landscape;

    // Use fast cached fonts
    final fonts = await _loadFontsConcurrently();
    final fontRegular = fonts[0];
    final fontBold = fonts[1];

    final pdf = pw.Document(
      theme: pw.ThemeData.withFont(base: fontRegular, bold: fontBold),
    );

    // Calculate valuation totals
    final int totalUnits = assets.fold<int>(0, (sum, a) => sum + a.quantity);
    final double grandTotalCost =
    assets.fold<double>(0.0, (sum, a) => sum + a.totalCost);

    const int rowsPerPage = 12;
    for (int i = 0; i < assets.length; i += rowsPerPage) {
      final pageAssets = assets.sublist(
        i,
        (i + rowsPerPage > assets.length) ? assets.length : i + rowsPerPage,
      );
      final isLastPage = (i + rowsPerPage) >= assets.length;

      pdf.addPage(
        pw.Page(
          pageFormat: pageFormat,
          margin: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(
            horizontal: 8.0 * PdfPageFormat.mm,
            vertical: 8.0 * PdfPageFormat.mm,
          ),
          build: (context) {
            return pw.Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                // Report Header
                _buildReportHeader(
                  title: 'PARISH PROPERTY & INVENTORY LISTING',
                  dateRangeLabel: dateRangeLabel,
                  sectionLabel: sectionLabel,
                  fontRegular: fontRegular,
                  fontBold: fontBold,
                  isLandscape: true,
                ),
                pw.SizedBox(height: 5),

                // Table Header + Rows (13 Columns)
                pw.Expanded(
                  child: pw.Table(
                    border: pw.TableBorder.all(
                      color: borderGrey,
                      width: 0.5,
                    ),
                    columnWidths: const {
                      0: pw.FlexColumnWidth(2.0), // Control Number
                      1: pw.FlexColumnWidth(2.6), // Item / Particulars
                      2: pw.FlexColumnWidth(1.8), // Location
                      3: pw.FlexColumnWidth(1.5), // Date of Inventory
                      4: pw.FlexColumnWidth(1.4), // Date Acquired
                      5: pw.FlexColumnWidth(1.5), // Mode of Acq
                      6: pw.FlexColumnWidth(0.8), // Quantity
                      7: pw.FlexColumnWidth(2.1), // Amount
                      8: pw.FlexColumnWidth(1.4), // Dimensions
                      9: pw.FlexColumnWidth(1.1), // Color
                      10: pw.FlexColumnWidth(1.5), // Model
                      11: pw.FlexColumnWidth(1.8), // Details / Markings
                      12: pw.FlexColumnWidth(2.2), // Remarks / Status
                    },
                    children: [
                      // Header Row
                      pw.TableRow(
                        decoration: const pw.BoxDecoration(color: marianBlue),
                        children: [
                          _buildTableHeaderCell('Control No.', fontBold),
                          _buildTableHeaderCell('Item / Particulars', fontBold),
                          _buildTableHeaderCell('Location', fontBold),
                          _buildTableHeaderCell('Date of Inv.', fontBold),
                          _buildTableHeaderCell('Date Acquired', fontBold),
                          _buildTableHeaderCell('Mode of Acq.', fontBold),
                          _buildTableHeaderCell('Qty', fontBold),
                          _buildTableHeaderCell('Amount (P)', fontBold),
                          _buildTableHeaderCell('Dimensions', fontBold),
                          _buildTableHeaderCell('Color', fontBold),
                          _buildTableHeaderCell('Model', fontBold),
                          _buildTableHeaderCell('Details / Markings', fontBold),
                          _buildTableHeaderCell('Remarks / Status', fontBold),
                        ],
                      ),

                      // Data Rows
                      ...pageAssets.map((asset) {
                        final inventoryDateStr = asset.lastAuditedAt != null
                            ? asset.formattedLastAuditedDate
                            : asset.formattedRegistrationDate;

                        return pw.TableRow(
                          decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
                            color: pageAssets.indexOf(asset) % 2 == 0
                                ? PdfColors.white
                                : lightCardBg,
                          ),
                          children: [
                            _buildTableCell(
                              asset.controlNumber,
                              fontBold,
                              textColor: marianBlue,
                            ),
                            _buildTableCell(asset.itemName, fontBold),
                            _buildTableCell(
                              asset.displayLocation,
                              fontRegular,
                            ),
                            _buildTableCell(
                              inventoryDateStr,
                              fontRegular,
                            ),
                            _buildTableCell(
                              asset.formattedAcquisitionDate,
                              fontRegular,
                            ),
                            _buildTableCell(
                              asset.modeOfAcquisition,
                              fontRegular,
                            ),
                            _buildTableCell(
                              '${asset.quantity}',
                              fontBold,
                              align: pw.TextAlign.center,
                            ),
                            _buildTableCell(
                              'Unit: P${AssetModel.formatCurrency(asset.unitPrice)}\nTotal: P${AssetModel.formatCurrency(asset.totalCost)}',
                              fontRegular,
                            ),
                            _buildTableCell(
                              asset.dimensions ?? '—',
                              fontRegular,
                            ),
                            _buildTableCell(
                              asset.color ?? '—',
                              fontRegular,
                            ),
                            _buildTableCell(
                              asset.model ?? '—',
                              fontRegular,
                            ),
                            _buildTableCell(
                              asset.others ?? '—',
                              fontRegular,
                            ),
                            _buildTableCell(
                              '${asset.conditionStatus.toUpperCase()} • ${asset.operationalStatus}\n${asset.remarks ?? ""}'
                                  .trim(),
                              fontRegular,
                            ),
                          ],
                        );
                      }),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),

                // Grand Totals & Signatories block on final page
                if (isLastPage) ...[
                  pw.SizedBox(height: 5),
                  pw.Container(
                    padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                      horizontal: 8,
                      vertical: 3.5,
                    ),
                    decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
                      color: lightCardBg,
                      border: pw.Border.all(color: borderGrey, width: 0.5),
                    ),
                    child: pw.Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                      children: [
                        pw.Text(
                          'TOTAL COUNT: ${assets.length} Item Line(s) • TOTAL QUANTITY: $totalUnits Unit(s)',
                          style: pw.TextStyle(
                            font: fontBold,
                            fontSize: 8.5,
                            color: marianBlue,
                          ),
                        ),
                        pw.Text(
                          'VALUATION GRAND TOTAL: P ${AssetModel.formatCurrency(grandTotalCost)}',
                          style: pw.TextStyle(
                            font: fontBold,
                            fontSize: 9.5,
                            color: PdfColors.green800,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  pw.SizedBox(height: 8),
                  _buildSignaturesBlock(fontRegular, fontBold),
                ],

                // Footer
                pw.SizedBox(height: 4),
                pw.Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    pw.Text(
                      'St. John Paul II Parish • Generated on ${DateTime.now().toLocal().toString().substring(0, 16)}',
                      style: pw.TextStyle(
                        font: fontRegular,
                        fontSize: 7.2,
                        color: textMuted,
                      ),
                    ),
                    pw.Text(
                      'Page ${context.pageNumber} of ${context.pagesCount}',
                      style: pw.TextStyle(
                        font: fontRegular,
                        fontSize: 7.2,
                        color: textMuted,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      );
    }

    return pdf.save();
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // Shared Header & Cell Helpers
  // ===========================================================================

  static pw.Widget _buildReportHeader({
    required String title,
    String? dateRangeLabel,
    String? sectionLabel,
    required pw.Font fontRegular,
    required pw.Font fontBold,
    bool isLandscape = false,
  }) {
    return pw.Container(
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 4),
      decoration: const pw.BoxDecoration(
        border: pw.Border(
          bottom: pw.BorderSide(color: goldAccent, width: 1.2),
        ),
      ),
      child: pw.Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.end,
        children: [
          pw.Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              pw.Text(
                'DIOCESE OF SAN PABLO • ST. JOHN PAUL II PARISH',
                style: pw.TextStyle(
                  font: fontBold,
                  fontSize: isLandscape ? 11 : 9.5,
                  color: marianNavy,
                  fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 1),
              pw.Text(
                title,
                style: pw.TextStyle(
                  font: fontBold,
                  fontSize: isLandscape ? 9.5 : 8.5,
                  color: marianBlue,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          pw.Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.end,
            children: [
              if (sectionLabel != null && sectionLabel.isNotEmpty)
                pw.Text(
                  'Scope: $sectionLabel',
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                    font: fontBold,
                    fontSize: 7.5,
                    color: PdfColors.black,
                  ),
                ),
              if (dateRangeLabel != null && dateRangeLabel.isNotEmpty)
                pw.Text(
                  'Range: $dateRangeLabel',
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                    font: fontRegular,
                    fontSize: 7.5,
                    color: textMuted,
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static pw.Widget _buildTableHeaderCell(String text, pw.Font fontBold) {
    return pw.Padding(
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 2.5, vertical: 3.5),
      child: pw.Text(
        text,
        style: pw.TextStyle(
          font: fontBold,
          fontSize: 6.8,
          color: PdfColors.white,
          fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
        ),
        textAlign: pw.TextAlign.center,
      ),
    );
  }

  static pw.Widget _buildTableCell(
      String text,
      pw.Font font, {
        PdfColor textColor = PdfColors.black,
        pw.TextAlign align = pw.TextAlign.left,
      }) {
    return pw.Padding(
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 2.5, vertical: 2.5),
      child: pw.Text(
        text,
        style: pw.TextStyle(
          font: font,
          fontSize: 6.5,
          color: textColor,
        ),
        textAlign: align,
      ),
    );
  }

  static pw.Widget _buildSignaturesBlock(
      pw.Font fontRegular, pw.Font fontBold) {
    return pw.Row(
      mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
      children: [
        pw.Expanded(
          child: pw.Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              pw.Container(
                width: 140,
                decoration: const pw.BoxDecoration(
                  border: pw.Border(
                    bottom: pw.BorderSide(color: PdfColors.black, width: 0.8),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 2),
              pw.Text(
                'Parish Secretary',
                style: pw.TextStyle(font: fontRegular, fontSize: 7.5),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        pw.Expanded(
          child: pw.Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              pw.Container(
                width: 140,
                decoration: const pw.BoxDecoration(
                  border: pw.Border(
                    bottom: pw.BorderSide(color: PdfColors.black, width: 0.8),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 2),
              pw.Text(
                'Parish Priest',
                style: pw.TextStyle(font: fontBold, fontSize: 7.5),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // Interactive Printing Triggers
  // ===========================================================================

  static Future<void> printPhotographicReport(
      List<AssetModel> assets, {
        String? dateRangeLabel,
        String? sectionLabel,
      }) async {
    final pdfBytes = await generatePhotographicReportPdf(
      assets,
      dateRangeLabel: dateRangeLabel,
      sectionLabel: sectionLabel,
    );
    await Printing.layoutPdf(
      onLayout: (format) async => pdfBytes,
      name:
      'Photographic_Inventory_Report_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
    );
  }

  static Future<void> printTableReport(
      List<AssetModel> assets, {
        String? dateRangeLabel,
        String? sectionLabel,
      }) async {
    final pdfBytes = await generateTableReportPdf(
      assets,
      dateRangeLabel: dateRangeLabel,
      sectionLabel: sectionLabel,
    );
    await Printing.layoutPdf(
      onLayout: (format) async => pdfBytes,
      name: 'Inventory_Ledger_Table_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
import '../../auth/services/auth_service.dart';
import '../models/asset_audit_log_model.dart';
import '../models/asset_model.dart';

class AssetService {
  static final SupabaseClient _client = Supabase.instance.client;

  static Future<List<AssetModel>> getAssets({
    bool includeArchived = false,
    String? categoryFilter,
    String? locationFilter,
    String? conditionFilter,
    String? sectionFilter,
  }) async {
    try {
      var query = _client.from('parish_assets').select('''
        *,
        asset_classifications (
          classification_id,
          acronym,
          classification_name
        ),
        asset_locations (
          location_id,
          acronym,
          location_name
        )
      ''');

      if (!includeArchived) {
        query = query
            .eq('is_archived', false)
            .neq('operational_status', 'Disposed')
            .neq('operational_status', 'Decommissioned')
            .neq('operational_status', 'Retired');
      }

      if (categoryFilter != null && categoryFilter != 'All') {
        query =
            query.eq('classification_acronym', categoryFilter.toUpperCase());
      }

      if (locationFilter != null && locationFilter != 'All') {
        query = query.eq('location_acronym', locationFilter.toUpperCase());
      }

      if (conditionFilter != null && conditionFilter != 'All') {
        query = query.eq(
            'condition_status', AssetModel.normalizeCondition(conditionFilter));
      }

      if (sectionFilter != null && sectionFilter != 'All') {
        if (sectionFilter.contains('Section 1') ||
            sectionFilter.contains('10,000.00 and Above')) {
          query = query.gte('unit_price', 10000.0);
        } else if (sectionFilter.contains('Section 2') ||
            sectionFilter.contains('Below 10,000.00')) {
          query = query.lt('unit_price', 10000.0);
        }
      }

      final response = await query
          .order('registration_date', ascending: false)
          .order('created_at', ascending: false);

      final List<dynamic> rows = response as List;
      return rows
          .map((r) => AssetModel.fromMap(r as Map<String, dynamic>))
          .toList();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error fetching parish assets: $e');
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  static Future<AssetModel?> findAssetByIdentifier(String identifier) async {
    final clean = identifier.trim();
    if (clean.isEmpty) return null;

    String baseIdentifier = clean;
    if (clean.contains('/')) {
      final parts = clean.split('/');
      if (parts.isNotEmpty && parts.first.trim().isNotEmpty) {
        baseIdentifier = parts.first.trim();
      }
    }

    try {
      final orFilter = [
        'control_number.eq.$clean',
        'asset_id.eq.$clean',
        'qr_code_token.eq.$clean',
        'rfid_tag.eq.$clean',
        if (baseIdentifier != clean) ...[
          'control_number.eq.$baseIdentifier',
          'asset_id.eq.$baseIdentifier',
          'qr_code_token.eq.$baseIdentifier',
        ]
      ].join(',');

      final response = await _client
          .from('parish_assets')
          .select('''
            *,
            asset_classifications (
              classification_id,
              acronym,
              classification_name
            ),
            asset_locations (
              location_id,
              acronym,
              location_name
            )
          ''')
          .or(orFilter)
          .maybeSingle();

      if (response != null) {
        return AssetModel.fromMap(response);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error querying asset identifier: $e');
    }
    return null;
  }

  static Future<List<AssetModel>> searchAssetSuggestions(
      String query, {
        int limit = 6,
      }) async {
    final clean = query.trim();
    if (clean.isEmpty) return [];

    final searchBase =
    clean.contains('/') ? clean.split('/').first.trim() : clean;

    try {
      final response = await _client
          .from('parish_assets')
          .select('''
            *,
            asset_classifications (
              classification_id,
              acronym,
              classification_name
            ),
            asset_locations (
              location_id,
              acronym,
              location_name
            )
          ''')
          .or('control_number.ilike.%$searchBase%,item_name.ilike.%$searchBase%,rfid_tag.ilike.%$searchBase%,model.ilike.%$searchBase%')
          .order('control_number', ascending: true)
          .limit(limit);

      return (response as List)
          .map((row) => AssetModel.fromMap(row as Map<String, dynamic>))
          .toList();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error searching asset suggestions: $e');
      return [];
    }
  }

  static Future<String?> uploadAssetPhoto({
    required String assetId,
    required Uint8List fileBytes,
    required String fileName,
  }) async {
    try {
      final cleanExt = fileName.contains('.')
          ? fileName.split('.').last.toLowerCase()
          : 'jpg';
      final storagePath =
          'asset_photos/$assetId/photo_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}.$cleanExt';

      await _client.storage.from('certificate-assets').uploadBinary(
        storagePath,
        fileBytes,
        fileOptions: FileOptions(
          contentType: 'image/$cleanExt',
          upsert: true,
        ),
      );

      return _client.storage
          .from('certificate-assets')
          .getPublicUrl(storagePath);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error uploading asset photo: $e');
      return null;
    }
  }

  static Future<int> getNextControlNumberSequence({
    required String locationAcronym,
    required String classificationAcronym,
    required int acquisitionYear,
  }) async {
    final locClean = locationAcronym.trim().toUpperCase();
    final clsClean = classificationAcronym.trim().toUpperCase();
    final prefix = '$locClean-$clsClean $acquisitionYear-';

    try {
      final List<dynamic> result = await _client.rpc(
        'generate_diocesan_asset_control_number_batch',
        params: {
          'p_location_acronym': locClean,
          'p_classification_acronym': clsClean,
          'p_acquisition_year': acquisitionYear,
          'p_count': 1,
        },
      );

      if (result.isNotEmpty) {
        final cNo =
            result.first['control_number']?.toString().trim() ?? '';
        final dashIdx = cNo.lastIndexOf('-');
        if (dashIdx != -1 && dashIdx < cNo.length - 1) {
          final parsed = int.tryParse(cNo.substring(dashIdx + 1));
          if (parsed != null && parsed > 0) return parsed;
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('RPC sequence lookup notice: $e. Falling back to table scan.');
    }

    try {
      final records = await _client
          .from('parish_assets')
          .select('control_number')
          .ilike('control_number', '$prefix%');

      int highestSeq = 0;
      for (final r in records) {
        final cNo = r['control_number']?.toString().trim() ?? '';
        final dashIndex = cNo.lastIndexOf('-');
        if (dashIndex != -1 && dashIndex < cNo.length - 1) {
          final parsed = int.tryParse(cNo.substring(dashIndex + 1));
          if (parsed != null && parsed > highestSeq) {
            highestSeq = parsed;
          }
        }
      }
      return highestSeq + 1;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error getting next control sequence: $e');
      return 1;
    }
  }

  static Future<bool> checkControlNumberExists(String controlNumber) async {
    final clean = controlNumber.trim();
    if (clean.isEmpty) return false;

    try {
      final response = await _client
          .from('parish_assets')
          .select('control_number')
          .ilike('control_number', clean)
          .maybeSingle();

      return response != null;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  static Future<List<String>> generateControlNumberBatch({
    required String locationAcronym,
    required String classificationAcronym,
    required int acquisitionYear,
    required int count,
  }) async {
    final safeCount = count > 0 ? count : 1;
    final locClean = locationAcronym.trim().toUpperCase();
    final clsClean = classificationAcronym.trim().toUpperCase();

    try {
      final List<dynamic> result = await _client.rpc(
        'generate_diocesan_asset_control_number_batch',
        params: {
          'p_location_acronym': locClean,
          'p_classification_acronym': clsClean,
          'p_acquisition_year': acquisitionYear,
          'p_count': safeCount,
        },
      );

      if (result.isNotEmpty) {
        return result
            .map((r) => r['control_number'].toString().trim())
            .toList();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Batch RPC notice: $e. Falling back to sequence scanner.');
    }

    final records = await _client
        .from('parish_assets')
        .select('control_number');

    int highestSeq = 0;
    for (final r in records) {
      final cNo = r['control_number']?.toString() ?? '';
      final dashIndex = cNo.lastIndexOf('-');
      if (dashIndex != -1 && dashIndex < cNo.length - 1) {
        final parsed = int.tryParse(cNo.substring(dashIndex + 1));
        if (parsed != null && parsed > highestSeq) {
          highestSeq = parsed;
        }
      }
    }

    final prefix = '$locClean-$clsClean $acquisitionYear-';
    final List<String> list = [];
    for (int i = 1; i <= safeCount; i++) {
      list.add('$prefix${(highestSeq + i).toString().padLeft(3, '0')}');
    }
    return list;
  }

  static String generatePermanentIdentifier(String prefix) {
    final rand = Random.secure();
    final values = List<int>.generate(8, (_) => rand.nextInt(256));
    final token = values
        .map((b) => b.toRadixString(16).padLeft(2, '0'))
        .join()
        .toUpperCase();
    return '$prefix-$token';
  }

  static Future<String?> _resolveValidUserId() async {
    String? currentUserId = AuthService.currentUser?.userId;
    if (currentUserId != null && currentUserId.isNotEmpty) {
      return currentUserId;
    }

    try {
      final defaultUser = await _client
          .from('users')
          .select('user_id')
          .eq('account_status', true)
          .limit(1)
          .maybeSingle();
      return defaultUser?['user_id'];
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /// Registers an asset. If month & day are optional, dateOfAcquisition is null
  /// and only acquisitionYear is recorded—never defaulting to January 1!
  static Future<AssetModel> registerAsset({
    required String itemName,
    required int quantity,
    required double unitPrice,
    required String classificationId,
    required String classificationAcronym,
    String? classificationName,
    required String locationId,
    required String locationAcronym,
    String? locationName,
    String? dimensions,
    String? color,
    String? model,
    String? others,
    String? remarks,
    String? photoUrl,
    DateTime? dateOfAcquisition,
    int? acquisitionYear,
    String modeOfAcquisition = 'Purchase',
    String? rfidTag,
    String conditionStatus = 'Good',
    String operationalStatus = 'In Storage',
    String? customControlNumber,
  }) async {
    final cleanItemName = itemName.trim();
    if (cleanItemName.isEmpty) throw 'Item name is required.';
    if (quantity <= 0) throw 'Quantity must be at least 1.';

    final validUserId = await _resolveValidUserId();
    final int finalAcqYear =
        acquisitionYear ?? (dateOfAcquisition?.year ?? DateTime.now().year);
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final normalizedStatus = AssetModel.normalizeStatus(operationalStatus);
    final bool isDecommissioned = normalizedStatus == 'Disposed';

    String assignedControlNumber;
    if (customControlNumber != null && customControlNumber.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      final cleanCustom = customControlNumber.trim().toUpperCase();
      final exists = await checkControlNumberExists(cleanCustom);
      if (exists) {
        throw 'Control Number "$cleanCustom" is already registered to another item. Please choose a different sequence number.';
      }
      assignedControlNumber = cleanCustom;
    } else {
      final controlNumbers = await generateControlNumberBatch(
        locationAcronym: locationAcronym,
        classificationAcronym: classificationAcronym,
        acquisitionYear: finalAcqYear,
        count: 1,
      );
      assignedControlNumber = controlNumbers.first;
    }

    final double safeUnitPrice = unitPrice >= 0.0 ? unitPrice : 0.0;
    final double calculatedTotalCost = quantity * safeUnitPrice;

    // Only format YYYY-MM-DD if date is specified; if optional, keep null
    final String? dateString = dateOfAcquisition != null
        ? '${dateOfAcquisition.year}-${dateOfAcquisition.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${dateOfAcquisition.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}'
        : null;

    final String assetId = generatePermanentIdentifier('AST');
    final String qrCodeToken = generatePermanentIdentifier('QR');

    final payload = {
      'asset_id': assetId,
      'control_number': assignedControlNumber,
      'item_name': cleanItemName,
      'classification_id': classificationId,
      'classification_acronym': classificationAcronym.trim().toUpperCase(),
      'location_id': locationId,
      'location_acronym': locationAcronym.trim().toUpperCase(),
      'dimensions':
      dimensions?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : dimensions!.trim(),
      'color': color?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : color!.trim(),
      'model': model?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : model!.trim(),
      'others': others?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : others!.trim(),
      'remarks': remarks?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : remarks!.trim(),
      'photo_url': photoUrl?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : photoUrl!.trim(),
      'date_of_acquisition': dateString,
      'acquisition_year': finalAcqYear,
      'mode_of_acquisition': modeOfAcquisition,
      'quantity': quantity,
      'unit_price': safeUnitPrice,
      'total_cost': calculatedTotalCost,
      'cost': calculatedTotalCost,
      'rfid_tag': (rfidTag != null && rfidTag.trim().isNotEmpty)
          ? rfidTag.trim()
          : null,
      'qr_code_token': qrCodeToken,
      'condition_status': AssetModel.normalizeCondition(conditionStatus),
      'operational_status': normalizedStatus,
      'is_archived': isDecommissioned,
      'archived_at': isDecommissioned ? now.toIso8601String() : null,
      'archived_by': isDecommissioned ? validUserId : null,
      'archive_reason': isDecommissioned
          ? 'Registered with Disposed availability.'
          : null,
      'bulk_batch_id': null,
      'item_sequence_in_batch': null,
      'registration_date': now.toIso8601String(),
      'created_by': validUserId,
      'created_at': now.toIso8601String(),
      'updated_at': now.toIso8601String(),
      'category': classificationName ?? classificationAcronym,
      'storage_location': locationName ?? locationAcronym,
      'acquisition_date': dateString,
      'acquisition_mode': modeOfAcquisition,
      'description': remarks ?? others ?? cleanItemName,
    };

    final response = await _client
        .from('parish_assets')
        .insert(payload)
        .select()
        .single();

    try {
      await _client.from('asset_audit_logs').insert({
        'audit_id': 'AUD-${now.millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
        'asset_id': assetId,
        'control_number': assignedControlNumber,
        'audited_by': validUserId,
        'previous_condition': null,
        'new_condition': AssetModel.normalizeCondition(conditionStatus),
        'previous_status': null,
        'new_status': normalizedStatus,
        'previous_location': null,
        'new_location': locationName ?? locationAcronym,
        'audit_method': 'MANUAL',
        'audit_notes':
        'Initial registration in parish inventory ($quantity unit(s)).',
        'audited_at': now.toIso8601String(),
      });
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Initial audit log notice: $e');
    }

    return AssetModel.fromMap(response);
  }

  static Future<AssetModel> updateAsset({
    required String assetId,
    required String itemName,
    String? classificationId,
    required String classificationAcronym,
    String? locationId,
    required String locationAcronym,
    String? locationName,
    String? dimensions,
    String? color,
    String? model,
    String? others,
    String? remarks,
    String? photoUrl,
    DateTime? dateOfAcquisition,
    int? acquisitionYear,
    required String modeOfAcquisition,
    int quantity = 1,
    required double unitPrice,
    String? rfidTag,
    required String conditionStatus,
    required String operationalStatus,
  }) async {
    final cleanItemName = itemName.trim();
    if (cleanItemName.isEmpty) throw 'Item name is required.';

    final now = DateTime.now();
    final int finalAcqYear =
        acquisitionYear ?? (dateOfAcquisition?.year ?? DateTime.now().year);
    final normalizedStatus = AssetModel.normalizeStatus(operationalStatus);
    final bool isDecommissioned = normalizedStatus == 'Disposed';
    final double safeUnitPrice = unitPrice >= 0.0 ? unitPrice : 0.0;
    final double safeTotalCost = quantity * safeUnitPrice;

    final String? dateString = dateOfAcquisition != null
        ? '${dateOfAcquisition.year}-${dateOfAcquisition.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${dateOfAcquisition.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}'
        : null;

    final updatePayload = {
      'item_name': cleanItemName,
      'classification_id': classificationId,
      'classification_acronym': classificationAcronym.trim().toUpperCase(),
      'location_id': locationId,
      'location_acronym': locationAcronym.trim().toUpperCase(),
      'dimensions':
      dimensions?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : dimensions!.trim(),
      'color': color?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : color!.trim(),
      'model': model?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : model!.trim(),
      'others': others?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : others!.trim(),
      'remarks': remarks?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : remarks!.trim(),
      if (photoUrl != null) 'photo_url': photoUrl.trim(),
      'date_of_acquisition': dateString,
      'acquisition_year': finalAcqYear,
      'mode_of_acquisition': modeOfAcquisition,
      'quantity': quantity > 0 ? quantity : 1,
      'unit_price': safeUnitPrice,
      'total_cost': safeTotalCost,
      'cost': safeTotalCost,
      'rfid_tag':
      rfidTag?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : rfidTag!.trim(),
      'condition_status': AssetModel.normalizeCondition(conditionStatus),
      'operational_status': normalizedStatus,
      'is_archived': isDecommissioned,
      'updated_at': now.toIso8601String(),
    };

    final response = await _client
        .from('parish_assets')
        .update(updatePayload)
        .eq('asset_id', assetId)
        .select()
        .single();

    return AssetModel.fromMap(response);
  }

  static Future<void> recordAuditScan({
    required String assetId,
    required String controlNumber,
    required String newCondition,
    String? previousCondition,
    required String newStatus,
    String? previousStatus,
    String? previousLocation,
    String? newLocation,
    String auditMethod = 'QR_SCAN',
    String? auditNotes,
    int? previousQuantity,
    int? newQuantity,
    String? quantityChangeReason,
    bool auditEntireGroup = false,
    List<String> groupAssetIds = const [],
    List<AssetQuantityDistribution>? quantityDistribution,
  }) async {
    final validUserId = await _resolveValidUserId();
    final now = DateTime.now();

    final bool hasQtyChange = previousQuantity != null &&
        newQuantity != null &&
        previousQuantity != newQuantity;

    String combinedNotes = '';

    if (hasQtyChange) {
      final change = newQuantity - previousQuantity;
      final sign = change > 0 ? '+$change' : '$change';
      final reason = (quantityChangeReason != null &&
          quantityChangeReason.trim().isNotEmpty)
          ? quantityChangeReason.trim()
          : 'Quantity updated during inspection';

      combinedNotes +=
      '[QTY_ADJUST: from $previousQuantity to $newQuantity ($sign) | REASON: $reason]\n';
    }

    if (quantityDistribution != null && quantityDistribution.isNotEmpty) {
      final jsonPayload =
      jsonEncode(quantityDistribution.map((d) => d.toMap()).toList());
      combinedNotes += '[QTY_DISTRIBUTION: $jsonPayload]\n';
    }

    if (auditNotes != null && auditNotes.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      combinedNotes += auditNotes.trim();
    }

    final finalNotes =
    combinedNotes.trim().isEmpty ? null : combinedNotes.trim();

    final normalizedNewCondition = AssetModel.normalizeCondition(newCondition);
    final normalizedNewStatus = AssetModel.normalizeStatus(newStatus);

    await _client.from('asset_audit_logs').insert({
      'audit_id': 'AUD-${now.millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
      'asset_id': assetId,
      'control_number': controlNumber,
      'audited_by': validUserId,
      'previous_condition': previousCondition,
      'new_condition': normalizedNewCondition,
      'previous_status': previousStatus,
      'new_status': normalizedNewStatus,
      'previous_location': previousLocation,
      'new_location': newLocation,
      'audit_method': auditMethod,
      'audit_notes': finalNotes,
      'audited_at': now.toIso8601String(),
    });

    final Map<String, dynamic> assetUpdates = {
      'condition_status': normalizedNewCondition,
      'operational_status': normalizedNewStatus,
      'last_audited_at': now.toIso8601String(),
      'audited_by': validUserId,
      'updated_at': now.toIso8601String(),
    };

    if (hasQtyChange && newQuantity > 0) {
      assetUpdates['quantity'] = newQuantity;

      try {
        final current = await _client
            .from('parish_assets')
            .select('unit_price')
            .eq('asset_id', assetId)
            .maybeSingle();

        if (current != null && current['unit_price'] != null) {
          final double uPrice =
              double.tryParse(current['unit_price'].toString()) ?? 0.0;
          assetUpdates['total_cost'] = newQuantity * uPrice;
          assetUpdates['cost'] = newQuantity * uPrice;
        }
      } catch (_) {}
    }

    await _client
        .from('parish_assets')
        .update(assetUpdates)
        .eq('asset_id', assetId);
  }

  static Future<List<AssetAuditLogModel>> getAssetAuditHistory(
      String assetId) async {
    try {
      final response = await _client
          .from('asset_audit_logs')
          .select('''
            *,
            users (
              first_name,
              last_name
            )
          ''')
          .eq('asset_id', assetId)
          .order('audited_at', ascending: false);

      return (response as List)
          .map((row) => AssetAuditLogModel.fromMap(row as Map<String, dynamic>))
          .toList();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error fetching audit history: $e');
      return [];
    }
  }

  static Future<void> archiveAsset({
    required String assetId,
    required String reason,
  }) async {
    final validUserId = await _resolveValidUserId();
    final now = DateTime.now();

    await _client.from('parish_assets').update({
      'is_archived': true,
      'archived_at': now.toIso8601String(),
      'archived_by': validUserId,
      'archive_reason': reason.trim(),
      'operational_status': 'Disposed',
      'updated_at': now.toIso8601String(),
    }).eq('asset_id', assetId);
  }

  static Future<void> restoreAsset(String assetId) async {
    await _client.from('parish_assets').update({
      'is_archived': false,
      'archived_at': null,
      'archived_by': null,
      'archive_reason': null,
      'operational_status': 'In Storage',
      'updated_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    }).eq('asset_id', assetId);
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// FILE NAME: asset_image_watermark_util.dart
// FILE PATH: lib/features/asset_inventory/utils/asset_image_watermark_util.dart
// =============================================================================

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/asset_inventory/utils/asset_image_watermark_util.dart
// =============================================================================

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'dart:ui' as ui;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AssetImageWatermarkUtil {
  AssetImageWatermarkUtil._();

  /// Standard export resolution boundaries (long edge)
  static const double minLongEdge = 1200.0;
  static const double maxLongEdge = 1600.0;

  /// Applies a full-frame diagonal tiled security watermark:
  /// - Text: `SJP2-DSP | <Diocesan Control Number>` (e.g. SJP2-DSP | C-FF 2026-0001)
  /// - Layout: Diagonal (-30°) tiled across the entire image area in staggered brick pattern
  /// - Opacity: 25% to 35% (calibrated ~28% white with subtle dark shadow for light scenes)
  /// - Export Resolution: Normalized to 1200–1600 pixels on the long edge
  static Future<Uint8List> applySecurityWatermark({
    required Uint8List rawImageBytes,
    String? assetIdentifier, // e.g. "C-FF 2026-0001" or "C-FF 2026-0001 • Wooden Pew"
    DateTime? captureTime,
  }) async {
    final Completer<ui.Image> completer = Completer();
    ui.decodeImageFromList(rawImageBytes, (ui.Image img) {
      completer.complete(img);
    });

    final ui.Image originalImage = await completer.future;
    final int origWidth = originalImage.width;
    final int origHeight = originalImage.height;

    // 1. Calculate Target Export Resolution (1200–1600 px on the long edge)
    final double longEdge = max(origWidth, origHeight).toDouble();
    double targetWidth = origWidth.toDouble();
    double targetHeight = origHeight.toDouble();

    if (longEdge > maxLongEdge) {
      final double scale = maxLongEdge / longEdge;
      targetWidth = (origWidth * scale).roundToDouble();
      targetHeight = (origHeight * scale).roundToDouble();
    } else if (longEdge < minLongEdge && longEdge > 0) {
      final double scale = minLongEdge / longEdge;
      targetWidth = (origWidth * scale).roundToDouble();
      targetHeight = (origHeight * scale).roundToDouble();
    }

    final ui.PictureRecorder recorder = ui.PictureRecorder();
    final Canvas canvas = Canvas(
      recorder,
      Rect.fromLTWH(0, 0, targetWidth, targetHeight),
    );

    // 2. Draw scaled background image
    final Rect srcRect = Rect.fromLTWH(
      0,
      0,
      origWidth.toDouble(),
      origHeight.toDouble(),
    );
    final Rect dstRect = Rect.fromLTWH(0, 0, targetWidth, targetHeight);

    canvas.drawImageRect(
      originalImage,
      srcRect,
      dstRect,
      Paint()..filterQuality = FilterQuality.high,
    );

    // 3. Extract and sanitize Diocesan Control Number
    String controlNo = 'PENDING-REG';
    if (assetIdentifier != null && assetIdentifier.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      final trimmed = assetIdentifier.trim();
      // If combined as "CONTROL-NO • Item Name", extract control number only
      if (trimmed.contains('•')) {
        controlNo = trimmed.split('•').first.trim().toUpperCase();
      } else {
        controlNo = trimmed.toUpperCase();
      }
    }

    // Format text: "SJP2-DSP | <CONTROL_NUMBER>"
    final String watermarkText = controlNo.startsWith('SJP2-DSP')
        ? controlNo
        : 'SJP2-DSP | $controlNo';

    // 4. Compute responsive font size based on scaled dimensions
    final double shortEdge = min(targetWidth, targetHeight);
    final double fontSize = (shortEdge * 0.032).clamp(18.0, 36.0);

    // 5. Build text painter with ~28% opacity white + subtle shadow for light images
    final TextPainter textPainter = TextPainter(
      text: TextSpan(
        text: watermarkText,
        style: TextStyle(
          fontSize: fontSize,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
          letterSpacing: 1.5,
          fontFamily: 'Courier',
          // ~28% opacity semi-transparent white (Color 0x48FFFFFF)
          color: const Color(0x48FFFFFF),
          shadows: const [
            // Dark shadow ensures readability on very light or pure white backgrounds
            Shadow(
              color: Color(0x28000000), // ~16% opacity dark shadow
              offset: Offset(1.0, 1.0),
              blurRadius: 2.0,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
    )..layout();

    // 6. Draw Full-Frame Diagonal Tiling (-30 degrees)
    canvas.save();

    // Rotate canvas around image center
    canvas.translate(targetWidth / 2, targetHeight / 2);
    const double rotationAngle = -pi / 6; // -30 degrees
    canvas.rotate(rotationAngle);

    // Bounding extent covering all corners after diagonal rotation
    final double diagonal = sqrt(
      (targetWidth * targetWidth) + (targetHeight * targetHeight),
    );

    final double textW = textPainter.width;
    final double textH = textPainter.height;

    // Spacing between repeated watermarks
    final double spacingX = textW * 0.55;
    final double spacingY = textH * 3.4;

    final double stepX = textW + spacingX;
    final double stepY = textH + spacingY;

    int row = 0;
    for (double y = -diagonal; y <= diagonal; y += stepY) {
      // Stagger alternating rows by half-width for cohesive security pattern
      final double rowOffset = (row % 2 == 0) ? 0.0 : (stepX / 2);

      for (double x = -diagonal - stepX; x <= diagonal + stepX; x += stepX) {
        textPainter.paint(canvas, Offset(x + rowOffset, y));
      }
      row++;
    }

    canvas.restore();

    // 7. Render final watermarked image
    final ui.Picture picture = recorder.endRecording();
    final ui.Image watermarkedImage = await picture.toImage(
      targetWidth.toInt(),
      targetHeight.toInt(),
    );
    final ByteData? byteData = await watermarkedImage.toByteData(
      format: ui.ImageByteFormat.png,
    );

    if (byteData == null) {
      return rawImageBytes;
    }

    return byteData.buffer.asUint8List();
  }

  /// Opens an interactive full-resolution image preview dialog showing the tiled watermark
  static void showImagePreviewModal(
      BuildContext context, {
        required ImageProvider imageProvider,
        required String title,
        String? controlNumber,
      }) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => Dialog(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        insetPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 24),
        child: Container(
          width: double.maxFinite,
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 860, maxHeight: 760),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF334155)),
            boxShadow: const [
              BoxShadow(
                color: Colors.black54,
                blurRadius: 24,
                offset: Offset(0, 10),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              // Header Banner
              Container(
                padding:
                const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 14),
                decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                  color: Color(0xFF1E293B),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    const Icon(
                      Icons.verified_outlined,
                      color: Color(0xFFD49B18),
                      size: 22,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            title,
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              color: Colors.white,
                              fontSize: 15,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            ),
                            maxLines: 1,
                            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          ),
                          if (controlNumber != null &&
                              controlNumber.isNotEmpty)
                            Text(
                              'Control #: $controlNumber • Tiled Anti-Tamper Watermark',
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                color: Color(0xFF94A3B8),
                                fontSize: 11.5,
                              ),
                            ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    IconButton(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.white70),
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),

              // Zoomable Image View
              Expanded(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                  child: InteractiveViewer(
                    minScale: 0.8,
                    maxScale: 4.0,
                    child: Center(
                      child: ClipRRect(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        child: Image(
                          image: imageProvider,
                          fit: BoxFit.contain,
                          loadingBuilder: (context, child, progress) {
                            if (progress == null) return child;
                            return const Center(
                              child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                                color: Colors.white,
                              ),
                            );
                          },
                          errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) {
                            return const Center(
                              child: Text(
                                'Failed to display image preview',
                                style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70),
                              ),
                            );
                          },
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),

              // Informational Footer
              Container(
                padding:
                const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 12),
                decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                  color: Color(0xFF1E293B),
                  borderRadius:
                  BorderRadius.vertical(bottom: Radius.circular(20)),
                ),
                child: const Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.security, size: 14, color: Color(0xFFD49B18)),
                    SizedBox(width: 6),
                    Text(
                      'Tiled security watermark embedded across the image for diocesan property protection.',
                      style:
                      TextStyle(fontSize: 11.5, color: Color(0xFF94A3B8)),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'web_camera_helper_stub.dart'
if (dart.library.html) 'web_camera_helper_web.dart';

class WebCameraHelper {
  WebCameraHelper._();

  /// Stops all active webcam hardware tracks in the browser DOM.
  static void releaseCamera() {
    stopAllWebCameraTracks();
  }

  /// Captures the current camera video frame directly to a byte array on Web.
  static Uint8List? captureFrame() {
    return captureCurrentWebVideoFrame();
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// FILE NAME: web_camera_helper_stub.dart
// FILE PATH: lib/features/asset_inventory/utils/web_camera_helper_stub.dart
// =============================================================================

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/asset_inventory/utils/web_camera_helper_stub.dart
// =============================================================================

import 'dart:typed_data';

/// Fallback for non-web platforms (Android, iOS, macOS, Windows, Linux)
void stopAllWebCameraTracks() {}

/// Fallback returning null on non-web platforms
Uint8List? captureCurrentWebVideoFrame() => null;

// =============================================================================
// FILE NAME: web_camera_helper_web.dart
// FILE PATH: lib/features/asset_inventory/utils/web_camera_helper_web.dart
// =============================================================================

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/asset_inventory/utils/web_camera_helper_web.dart
// =============================================================================

import 'dart:convert';
// ignore: avoid_web_libraries_in_flutter
import 'dart:html' as html;
import 'dart:typed_data';

/// Scans the browser DOM for active video stream tracks and explicitly stops them
/// so Chrome, Edge, Safari, and Firefox turn off the webcam light and camera icon.
void stopAllWebCameraTracks() {
  try {
    final videoElements = html.document.querySelectorAll('video');
    for (final element in videoElements) {
      if (element is html.VideoElement) {
        final stream = element.srcObject;
        if (stream is html.MediaStream) {
          final tracks = stream.getTracks();
          for (final track in tracks) {
            try {
              track.stop();
            } catch (_) {}
          }
          element.srcObject = null;
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (_) {}
}

/// Captures the current visible frame from any active HTML video element in the DOM
/// and returns it as a JPEG encoded Uint8List directly in browser memory without
/// triggering page reloads, tab suspension, or navigation resets.
Uint8List? captureCurrentWebVideoFrame() {
  try {
    final videoElements = html.document.querySelectorAll('video');
    for (final element in videoElements) {
      if (element is html.VideoElement) {
        final int w = element.videoWidth > 0
            ? element.videoWidth
            : (element.clientWidth > 0 ? element.clientWidth : 640);
        final int h = element.videoHeight > 0
            ? element.videoHeight
            : (element.clientHeight > 0 ? element.clientHeight : 480);

        if (w > 0 && h > 0) {
          final canvas = html.CanvasElement(width: w, height: h);
          final ctx = canvas.context2D;
          ctx.drawImageScaled(element, 0, 0, w, h);
          final dataUrl = canvas.toDataUrl('image/jpeg', 0.92);
          final commaIndex = dataUrl.indexOf(',');
          if (commaIndex != -1) {
            final base64String = dataUrl.substring(commaIndex + 1);
            return base64Decode(base64String);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (_) {}
  return null;
}
